).

•

The free variables of any method are a subset of its parameters.

•

The special variable
result
is not a free variable of any method.

•

No parameters appear on the left side of a variable assignment.

•

Formulas in pre-conditions only reference parameters.

•

Formulas in post-conditions only reference parameters and the special variable
result
.

•

If a pre-condition is imprecise, the post-condition is also imprecise.

Appendix B
Run-time Semantics

B.1.
Definitions

The rules in the following section reference an ambient program with elements denoted as follows:

•

Predicates:
p\in\textsc{Predicate}

•

Methods:
m\in\textsc{Method}

•

Structs:
S\in\textsc{Struct}

•

Types:
T\in\textsc{Type}

•

Variables:
x\in\textsc{Var}

•

Field identifiers:
f\in\textsc{Field}

•

Locations (opaque values):
\ell\in\textsc{Location}

•

Literals (integers, characters, booleans, null):
l\in\textsc{Literal}

•

Values:
v\in\textsc{Value}=\textsc{Location}\cup\textsc{Literal}

•

Gradual formulas:
\tilde{\phi}\in\tilde{\textsc{F}}\textsc{ormula}

•

Precise formulas:
\phi\in\textsc{Formula}

•

Statements:
s\in\textsc{Stmt}

•

Heap:
H:\textsc{Location}\times\textsc{Field}\to\textsc{Value}

•

Permissions:
\alpha\in\mathcal{P}(\textsc{Location}\times\textsc{Field})

•

Environment:
\rho:\textsc{Var}\rightharpoonup\textsc{Value}

The following functions are defined to access elements in the program:

•

\operatorname{default}:\textsc{Type}\to\textsc{Value}
– Gets the default value of the given type (
0
,
null
, etc.)

•

\operatorname{pre}:\textsc{Method}\to\tilde{\textsc{F}}\textsc{ormula}
– Gets the precondition from the declaration of the specified method.

•

\operatorname{post}:\textsc{Method}\to\tilde{\textsc{F}}\textsc{ormula}
– Gets the postcondition from the declaration of the specified method.

•

\operatorname{body}:\textsc{Method}\to\textsc{Stmt}
– Gets the body from the declaration of the specified method.

•

\operatorname{params}:\textsc{Method}\to\overline{\textsc{Var}}
– Gets the list of parameters from the declaration of the specified predicate.

•

\operatorname{predicate}:\textsc{Predicate}\to\tilde{\textsc{F}}\textsc{ormula}
– Gets the body from the declaration of the specified predicate.

•

\operatorname{predicate\_params}:\textsc{Predicate}\to\overline{\textsc{Var}}
– Gets the list of parameters from the declaration of the specified predicate.

•

\operatorname{struct}:\textsc{Struct}\to\overline{\textsc{Field}}
– Gets the list of fields from the declaration of the specified struct.

B.2.
Evaluation

The relation

\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow v

denotes the evaluation of an expression
e\in\textsc{Expr}
to a value
v\in\textsc{Value}

where
H:\textsc{Value}\times\textsc{Field}\to\textsc{Value}
represents the heap, and
\rho:\textsc{Var}\rightharpoonup\textsc{Value}
represents the local variable environment.

\frac{}{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash l\Downarrow l}\quad\textsc{EvalLiteral}

\frac{}{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash x\Downarrow\rho(x)}\quad\textsc{EvalVar}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{false}}{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}e_{2}\Downarrow\texttt{false}}\quad\textsc{EvalAndA}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{true}\quad\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{2}\Downarrow v_{2}}{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}e_{2}\Downarrow v_{2}}\quad\textsc{EvalAndB}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{true}}{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}\Downarrow\texttt{true}}\quad\textsc{EvalOrA}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{false}\quad\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{2}\Downarrow v_{2}}{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}\Downarrow v_{2}}\quad\textsc{EvalOrB}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow v_{1}\quad\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{2}\Downarrow v_{2}}{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\oplus e_{2}\Downarrow v_{1}\oplus v_{2}}\quad\textsc{EvalOp}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow v}{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash\mathop{\texttt{!}}e\Downarrow\neg v}\quad\textsc{EvalNeg}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\ell}{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e.f\Downarrow H(\ell,f)}\quad\textsc{EvalField}

B.3.
Formulas

Formula
is the set of all
\phi
elements in the grammar, while
\tilde{\textsc{F}}\textsc{ormula}
is the set of all
\tilde{\phi}
elements in the grammar.

Definition 0.

An
imprecise
formula
\tilde{\phi}
is any formula in
\tilde{\textsc{F}}\textsc{ormula}
of the form
\texttt{?}*\phi
where
\phi\in\textsc{Formula}
.

Otherwise, a formula
\phi
is
precise
and
\phi\in\textsc{Formula}
.

Definition 0.

A formula
\tilde{\phi}
is
completely precise
if there is no
H,\alpha,\rho
such that
AssertImprecise
applies at some step in the derivation of
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}
.

In other words, a completely precise formula is precise and all predicate bodies referenced in its equi-recursive unrolling are also precise.

Definition 0.

A formula
\tilde{\phi}
is a
specification
if either
\tilde{\phi}
is imprecise or
\tilde{\phi}
is precise and self-framed (definition
6
).

B.4.
Footprints

Definition 0.

The
exact footprint
of a formula
\tilde{\phi}\in\tilde{\textsc{F}}\textsc{ormula}
, denoted
\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}
, or of an expression
e
, denoted
\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}
, is the set of permissions that must be accessed when asserting
\tilde{\phi}
or evaluating
e
.

By lemmas
4
and
11
, if
\tilde{\phi}
is a specification, this set is the lower bound of permissions that satisfy
\tilde{\phi}
.

The calculation of exact footprints is defined as follows:

\displaystyle\llfloor l\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\emptyset

\displaystyle\llfloor x\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\emptyset

\displaystyle\llfloor e.f\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle};\langle\ell,\,f\rangle

\displaystyle\text{if }\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\ell

\displaystyle\llfloor e_{1}\oplus e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor e_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\cup\llfloor e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle\llfloor e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor e_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle\text{if }\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{true}

\displaystyle\llfloor e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor e_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\cup\llfloor e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle\text{if }\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{false}

\displaystyle\llfloor e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor e_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle\text{if }\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{false}

\displaystyle\llfloor e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor e_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\cup\llfloor e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle\text{if }\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{true}

\displaystyle\llfloor\mathop{\texttt{!}}e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle\llfloor\texttt{?}*\phi\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor\phi\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle\llfloor\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor\phi_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\cup\llfloor\phi_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle\llfloor p(\overline{e})\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,[\overline{x\mapsto v}]\rangle}~\cup

\displaystyle\text{if }\overline{x}=\operatorname{predicate\_params}(p)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\bigcup\overline{\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}}

\displaystyle\text{and }\overline{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow v}

\displaystyle\llfloor\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\cup\llfloor\phi_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle\text{if }\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{true}

\displaystyle\llfloor\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\cup\llfloor\phi_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle\text{if }\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{false}

\displaystyle\llfloor\texttt{acc}(e.f)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle};\langle\ell,\,f\rangle

\displaystyle\text{if }\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\ell

Definition 0.

The
maximal footprint
of a formula, denoted
\lfloor\tilde{\phi}\rfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle}
, is the set of all permissions that
\tilde{\phi}
may represent in the context of a heap
H
, permission set
\alpha
, and variable environment
\rho
.

The footprint of a completely precise formula (definition
2
) is its exact footprint, while the footprint of a formula which is not completely precise is the current set of permissions.

\lfloor\tilde{\phi}\rfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle}:=\begin{cases}\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}&\text{if $\tilde{\phi}$ is completely precise}\\
\alpha&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

B.5.
Framing

The relation
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
denotes that
e\in\textsc{Expr}
is framed by the permissions contained in
\alpha\in\mathcal{P}(\textsc{Perm})
.

\frac{}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}l}\quad\textsc{FrameLiteral}

\frac{}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}x}\quad\textsc{FrameVar}

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{acc}(e.f)}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e.f}\quad\textsc{FrameField}

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{2}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\oplus e_{2}}\quad\textsc{FrameOp}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{true}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}}\quad\textsc{FrameOrA}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{false}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{2}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}}\quad\textsc{FrameOrB}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{false}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}e_{2}}\quad\textsc{FrameAndA}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{true}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{2}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}e_{2}}\quad\textsc{FrameAndB}

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}\mathop{\texttt{!}}e}\quad\textsc{FrameNeg}

The relation
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\phi
denotes that
\phi\in\textsc{Formula}
is framed by the permissions in
\alpha\in\mathcal{P}(\textsc{Perm})
using an iso-recursive interpretation of predicates (i.e., without unrolling predicate instances).

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}e}\quad\textsc{IFrameExpression}

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\phi_{1}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\phi_{2}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}}\quad\textsc{IFrameConjunction}

\frac{\overline{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}p(\overline{e})}\quad\textsc{IFramePredicate}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{true}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\phi_{1}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}}\quad\textsc{IFrameConditionalA}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{false}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\phi_{2}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}}\quad\textsc{IFrameConditionalB}

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\texttt{acc}(e.f)}\quad\textsc{IFrameAcc}

Define the relation
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\phi
denotes that
\phi\in\textsc{Formula}
is framed by the permissions in
\alpha\in\mathcal{P}(\textsc{Perm})
using an equi-recursive interpretation of predicates (i.e., unrolling predicate instances).

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}e}\quad\textsc{EFrameExpression}

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\phi_{1}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\phi_{2}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}}\quad\textsc{EFrameConjunction}

\frac{\overline{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}\quad\overline{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow v}\quad\overline{x}=\operatorname{predicate\_params}(p)\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,[\overline{x\mapsto v}]\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\operatorname{predicate}(p)}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}p(\overline{e})}\quad\textsc{EFramePredicate}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{true}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\phi_{1}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}}\quad\textsc{EFrameConditionalA}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{false}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\phi_{2}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}}\quad\textsc{EFrameConditionalB}

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\texttt{acc}(e.f)}\quad\textsc{EFrameAcc}

Definition 0.

A
self-framed
formula is a precise formula
\phi\in\textsc{Formula}
such that for all
H,\alpha,\rho
,

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi\implies\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\phi.

B.6.
Assertions

The relation
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}
denotes the validity of
\tilde{\phi}\in\tilde{\textsc{F}}\textsc{ormula}
for the state represented by
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle
.

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\phi}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{?}*\phi}\quad\textsc{AssertImprecise}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{true}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\mathit{e}}\quad\textsc{AssertValue}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{true}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}}\quad\textsc{AssertIfA}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{false}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{2}}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}}\quad\textsc{AssertIfB}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\ell\quad\langle\ell,\,f\rangle\in\alpha}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{acc}(e.f)}\quad\textsc{AssertAcc}

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha_{1},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha_{2},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{2}\quad\alpha_{1}\cup\alpha_{2}\subseteq\alpha\quad\alpha_{1}\cap\alpha_{2}=\emptyset}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}}\quad\textsc{AssertConjunction}

\frac{\overline{x}=\operatorname{predicate\_params}(p)\quad\overline{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow v}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,[\overline{x\mapsto v}]\rangle\vDash\operatorname{predicate}(p)}{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash p(\overline{e})}\quad\textsc{AssertPredicate}

B.7.
Execution

Definition 0.

A
stack

\mathcal{S}
is a list of the form

\langle\alpha_{n},\,\rho_{n},\,s_{n}\rangle\cdot\ldots\cdot\langle\alpha_{1},\,\rho_{1},\,s_{1}\rangle\cdot\mathsf{nil}

where
n\geq 1
,
\alpha_{n},\cdots,\alpha_{1}
are permission sets,
\rho_{n},\cdots,\rho_{1}
are variable environments, and
s_{n},\cdots,s_{1}
are statements.

\alpha(\mathcal{S})
,
\rho(\mathcal{S})
, and
s(\mathcal{S})
may be used to denote the values
\alpha_{n}
,
\rho_{n}
, and
s_{n}
, respectively.

Definition 0.

An
exclusion frame

\hat{\alpha}
a set of permissions that may not be transferred to a callee stack frame. This is necessary to ensure that the permissions represented by the imprecise specifications of a callee cannot overlap with some predicate instance that is owned by the caller.

Small-step execution is denoted by the judgement

\langle H,\,\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H^{\prime},\,\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle

for stacks
\mathcal{S},\mathcal{S}^{\prime}
, heap
H
, and exclusion frame
\hat{\alpha}
.

\frac{}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{skip}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecSeq}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow v\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,x=e\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho[x\mapsto v],\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecAssign}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash x\Downarrow\ell\quad\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow v\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{acc}(x.f)\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e\quad H^{\prime}=H[\langle\ell,\,f\rangle\mapsto v]}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,x.f=e\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H^{\prime},\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecAssignField}

\frac{\ell=\operatorname{fresh}\quad\overline{T~f}=\operatorname{struct}(S)\quad H^{\prime}=H[\overline{\langle\ell,\,f\rangle\mapsto\operatorname{default}(T)}]\quad\alpha^{\prime}=\alpha\cup\{\overline{\langle\ell,\,f\rangle}\}}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,x=\texttt{alloc}(S)\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H^{\prime},\,\langle\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho[x\mapsto\ell],\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecAlloc}

\frac{\overline{x}=\operatorname{params}(m)\quad\overline{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow v}\quad\overline{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}\quad\rho^{\prime}=[\overline{x\mapsto v}]\quad\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\hat{\alpha},\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle\vDash\operatorname{pre}(m)\quad\alpha^{\prime}=\lfloor\operatorname{pre}(m)\rfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\hat{\alpha},\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle}}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,y\mathbin{\texttt{=}}m(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\\
\\
\to\\
\\
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho^{\prime},\,\operatorname{body}(m)\texttt{;}~\texttt{skip}\rangle\cdot\langle\alpha\setminus\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho,\,y\mathbin{\texttt{=}}m(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecCallEnter}

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle\vDash\operatorname{post}(m)\quad\rho^{\prime\prime}=\rho[y\mapsto\rho^{\prime}(\texttt{result})]\quad\alpha^{\prime\prime}=\alpha\cup\lfloor\operatorname{post}(m)\rfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle}}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho^{\prime},\,\texttt{skip}\rangle\cdot\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,y\mathbin{\texttt{=}}m(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha^{\prime\prime},\,\rho^{\prime\prime},\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecCallExit}

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{?}*\phi}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{assert}~\tilde{\phi}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecAssert}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{true}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}{\langle H,\,\langle H,\,\rho,\,\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~s_{1}~\texttt{else}~s_{2}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H,\,\langle H,\,\rho,\,s_{1}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecIfA}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{false}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}{\langle H,\,\langle H,\,\rho,\,\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~s_{1}~\texttt{else}~s_{2}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H,\,\langle H,\,\rho,\,s_{2}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecIfB}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{true}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e\quad\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\hat{\alpha},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}\quad\alpha^{\prime}=\lfloor\tilde{\phi}\rfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\hat{\alpha},\,\rho\rangle}}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s^{\prime}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\\
\\
\to\\
\\
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho,\,s^{\prime}\texttt{;}~\texttt{skip}\rangle\cdot\langle\alpha\setminus\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s^{\prime}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecWhileEnter}

\frac{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{false}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e\quad\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\hat{\alpha},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s^{\prime}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecWhileSkip}

\frac{\langle H,\,\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}\quad\alpha^{\prime\prime}=\alpha\cup\lfloor\tilde{\phi}\rfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle}}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho^{\prime},\,\texttt{skip}\rangle\cdot\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s^{\prime}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\\
\\
\to\\
\\
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha^{\prime\prime},\,\rho^{\prime},\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s^{\prime}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecWhileFinish}

\frac{}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{fold}~p(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot S\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot S\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecFold}

\frac{}{\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{unfold}~p(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot S\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot S\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecUnfold}

B.8.
Reachable transitions

Definition 0.

An
execution state

\Gamma
is either one of the abstract symbols
\mathsf{final}
or
\mathsf{init}
, or a pair
\langle H,\,\mathcal{S}\rangle
of a heap
H
and a stack
\mathcal{S}
.

Definition 0.

An execution state
\Gamma
is
well-formed
if
\Gamma
is either one of the abstract symbols
\mathsf{init}
or
\mathsf{final}
, or of the form
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha_{n},\,\rho_{n},\,s_{n}\rangle\cdot\ldots\cdot\langle\alpha_{1},\,\rho_{1},\,s_{1}\rangle\cdot\mathsf{nil}\rangle
and

•

\alpha_{i}\cap\alpha_{j}=\emptyset
for all
1\leq i<j\leq n
.

•

s_{n}=s\texttt{;}~\texttt{skip}
for some statement
s
or
s_{n}=\texttt{skip}
.

•

For all
1\leq i<n
,
s_{i}=s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime}\texttt{;}~\texttt{skip}
or
s_{i}=s\texttt{;}~\texttt{skip}
for some statements
s
and
s^{\prime}
where
s
is of the form
m(\overline{e})
for some
m
,
\overline{e}
or
\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s_{body}
for some
e
,
\tilde{\phi}
,
s_{body}
.

Examining the execution rules shows that well-formedness of states is preserved by the execution rules defined above.

A dynamic execution transition
\Gamma\to\Gamma^{\prime}
is reachable under a program
\Pi
, using the exclusion frame
\hat{\alpha}
, when the following judgement holds:

\Pi\vdash\Gamma,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\Gamma^{\prime}

\frac{}{\langle s,\,M,\,P,\,S\rangle\vdash\mathsf{init},\,\_\to\langle\emptyset,\,\langle\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathsf{nil}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecInit}

\frac{\Pi\vdash\_,\,\_\to\langle H,\,\mathcal{S}\rangle\quad\langle H,\,\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H^{\prime},\,\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle}{\Pi\vdash\langle H,\,\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H^{\prime},\,\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle}\quad\textsc{ExecStep}

\frac{}{\Pi\vdash\langle\_,\,\langle\_,\,\_,\,\texttt{skip}\rangle\cdot\mathsf{nil}\rangle,\,\_\to\mathsf{final}}\quad\textsc{ExecFinal}

Definition 0.

An execution state
\Gamma
is
reachable
from program
\Pi
if
\Gamma=\mathsf{init}
or
\Pi\vdash\_,\,\_\to\Gamma
.

Appendix C
Symbolic Execution

C.1.
Definitions

Definition 0.

A
symbolic value

\nu\in\textsc{SValue}
is an abstract value that represents an unknown character, boolean, integer, or location value. We leave the concrete type of
SValue
undefined, but assume that an infinite number of distinct new values can be produced by the
\operatorname{fresh}
function.

Definition 0.

A
symbolic expression

t\in\textsc{SExpr}
is a symbolic value or symbolic expressions combined using operators. Note that the binary operators
\oplus
are the same as in §
A
.

t::=\nu\quad|\quad l\quad|\quad\mathop{\texttt{!}}t\quad|\quad t_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t_{2}\quad|\quad t_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}t_{2}\quad|\quad t_{1}\oplus t_{2}

Definition 0.

A
path condition

g\in\textsc{SExpr}
is a symbolic expression consisting of conjuncts added at every branch point during a particular symbolic execution path.

Definition 0.

An
imprecise flag

\iota\in\{\top,\bot\}
is a flag that indicates whether a state is imprecise.

Definition 0.

A
symbolic evironment

\gamma:\textsc{Var}\rightharpoonup\textsc{SExpr}
is a partial function mapping variable names to symbolic expressions.

Definition 0.

A
field chunk

\langle f,\,t,\,t^{\prime}\rangle\in\textsc{SField}
denotes the mapping of the field
f
of instance
t
to the value
t^{\prime}
.

Definition 0.

A
predicate chunk

\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\in\textsc{SPredicate}
represents an isorecursive predicate
p
with symbolically-evaluated arguments
\overline{t}
.

Definition 0.

A
heap chunk

h\in\textsc{SField}\cup\textsc{SPredicate}
is either a field chunk or a predicate chunk.

Definition 0.

A
precise symbolic heap
(usually abbreviated as precise heap)
\mathsf{H}\in\mathcal{P}(\textsc{SField}\cup\textsc{SPredicate})
is a set of heap chunks where all heap chunks must occupy distinct heap locations at run time.

Definition 0.

An
optimistic symbolic heap
(usually abbreviated as optimistic heap)
\mathcal{H}\in\mathcal{P}(\textsc{SField})
is a set of field chunks where distinct chunks may coincide on the heap at run time (i.e. object references that are distinct symbolic expressions may be represent the same object value at run time).

Definition 0.

A
symbolic permission

\theta\in\textsc{SPerm}
represents a particular heap location or predicate instance.

\theta::=\langle f,\,t\rangle\quad|\quad\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle

A set of symbolic permissions is denoted by
\Theta
.

Definition 0.

A
run-time check

r\in\textsc{SCheck}
is a symbolic value that must be asserted at run time, a symbolic permission whose access must be asserted at run time, a set of symbolic permissions whose disjointness must be asserted at run time, or an unsatisfiable check.

r::=t\quad|\quad\theta\quad|\quad\operatorname{sep}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})\quad|\quad\bot

A set of run-time checks is denoted by
\mathcal{R}
.

Definition 0.

A
symbolic state

\sigma\in\textsc{SState}
consists of an imprecise flag, a path condition, a precise heap, an optimistic heap, and a symbolic environment.

\sigma::=\langle\iota,\,g,\,\mathsf{H},\,\mathcal{H},\,\gamma\rangle

\iota(\sigma)
,
g(\sigma)
,
\mathsf{H}(\sigma)
,
\mathcal{H}(\sigma)
, and
\gamma(\sigma)
each denote a reference to the respective component of
\sigma
.

C.2.
Valuations

In order to prove soundness with respect to the dynamic semantics, we must first define a correspondence between the two representations.

Definition 0.

A
valuation

V:\textsc{SValue}\rightharpoonup\textsc{Value}
is a partial function mapping symbolic values to concrete values.

A base valuation
V:\textsc{SValue}\rightharpoonup\textsc{Value}
is implicitly extended to
V:\textsc{SExpr}\rightharpoonup\textsc{Value}
for all possible symbolic expressions composed of literals and symbolic values in the domain of
V
:

\displaystyle V(l)

\displaystyle:=l

\displaystyle V(t_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{+}}t_{2})

\displaystyle:=V(t_{1})+V(t_{2})

\displaystyle V(t_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{-}}t_{2})

\displaystyle:=V(t_{1})-V(t_{2})

\displaystyle V(t_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{*}}t_{2})

\displaystyle:=V(t_{1})\cdot V(t_{2})

\displaystyle V(t_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{/}}t_{2})

\displaystyle:=\frac{V(t_{1})}{V(t_{2})}

\displaystyle V(t_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{2})

\displaystyle:=\begin{cases}\texttt{true}&\text{if}~V(t_{1})=V(t_{2})\\
\texttt{false}&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

\displaystyle V(\mathop{\texttt{!}}t)

\displaystyle:=\begin{cases}\texttt{true}&\text{if}~V(t_{1})=\texttt{false}\\
\texttt{false}&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

\displaystyle V(t_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}t_{2})

\displaystyle:=\begin{cases}\texttt{true}&\text{if}~V(t_{1})=\texttt{true}~\text{or}~V(t_{2})=\texttt{true}\\
\texttt{false}&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

\displaystyle V(t_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t_{2})

\displaystyle:=\begin{cases}\texttt{true}&\text{if}~V(t_{1})=\texttt{true}~\text{and}~V(t_{2})=\texttt{true}\\
\texttt{false}&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

Definition 0.

A symbolic expression
t_{1}

implies
another symbolic expression
t_{2}
(denoted
t_{1}\implies t_{2}
) if, for all valuations for which
V(t_{1})
and
V(t_{2})
are defined,
(V(t_{1})=\texttt{true})\implies(V(t_{2})=\texttt{true})
.

C.3.
Footprints

V\llparenthesis\theta\rrparenthesis_{H}
and
V\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H}
denote the footprint (i.e. set of permissions) necessary to satisfy the given symbolic permission or symbolic permission set, respectively, given some heap
H
.

\displaystyle V\llparenthesis\langle f,\,t\rangle\rrparenthesis_{H}

\displaystyle:=\langle V(t),\,f\rangle

\displaystyle V\llparenthesis\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\rrparenthesis_{H}

\displaystyle:=\llfloor\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,[\overline{x\mapsto V(t)}]\rangle}

\displaystyle V\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H}

\displaystyle:=\bigcup_{\theta\in\Theta}V\llparenthesis\theta\rrparenthesis_{H}

C.4.
Correspondence

The relations
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}
,
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathcal{H}
,
\rho\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\gamma
, and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma
denote correspondence between symbolic states and run-time states:

(1)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{\iff}}&(\forall\,\langle f,\,t,\,t^{\prime}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}:H(V(t),f)=V(t^{\prime}))~\wedge\\
&(\forall\,\langle f,\,t,\,t^{\prime}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}:\langle V(t),\,f\rangle\in\alpha)~\wedge\\
&(\forall\,\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}:\langle H,\,\alpha,\,[\overline{x\mapsto V(t)}]\rangle\vDash\operatorname{predicate}(p))~\wedge\\
&(\forall\,h_{1},h_{2}\in\mathsf{H}^{2}:h_{1}\neq h_{2}\implies V\llparenthesis h_{1}\rrparenthesis_{H}\cap V\llparenthesis h_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H}=\emptyset)\end{split}

(2)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathcal{H}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{\iff}}&(\forall\,\langle f,\,t,\,t^{\prime}\rangle\in\mathcal{H}:H(V(t),f)=V(t^{\prime}))~\wedge\\
&(\forall\,\langle f,\,t,\,t^{\prime}\rangle\in\mathcal{H}:\langle V(t),\,f\rangle\in\alpha)\end{split}

(3)

\displaystyle\rho\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\gamma\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{\iff}}

\displaystyle\forall\,x\in\operatorname{dom}(\gamma):\rho(x)=V(t)

(4)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{\iff}}&(\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}(\sigma))~\wedge\\
&(\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathcal{H}(\sigma))~\wedge\\
&(\rho\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\gamma(\sigma))~\wedge\\
&(V(g(\sigma))=\texttt{true})\end{split}

C.5.
Run-time checks

The judgement
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V}r
denotes that a symbolic runtime check
r\in\textsc{SCheck}
is satisfied at run time by a heap
H
and permission set
\alpha
through a valuation
V
. Note that there is no rule for
\bot
; by design it can never be satisfied.

\frac{V(t)=\texttt{true}}{\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V}t}\quad\textsc{CheckValue}

\frac{\langle V(t),\,f\rangle\in\alpha}{\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V}\langle f,\,t\rangle}\quad\textsc{CheckAcc}

\frac{\overline{x}=\operatorname{predicate\_params}(p)\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,[\overline{x\mapsto V(t)}]\rangle\vDash\operatorname{predicate}(p)}{\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V}\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle}\quad\textsc{CheckPred}

\frac{V\llparenthesis\Theta_{1}\rrparenthesis_{H}\cap V\llparenthesis\Theta_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H}=\emptyset}{\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V}\operatorname{sep}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})}\quad\textsc{CheckSep}

This judgement is naturally extended for a set of runtime checks
\mathcal{R}
:

\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V}\mathcal{R}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{\iff}}\forall\,r\in\mathcal{R}:\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V}r

C.6.
Evaluation

The judgement

\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}

denotes the evaluation of the expression
e\in\textsc{Expr}
in the symbolic state
\sigma\in\textsc{SState}
. It yields the symbolic expression
t\in\textsc{SExpr}
, a new symbolic state
\sigma^{\prime}
which must be satisfied to produce the resulting value, and a set of run-time checks
\mathcal{R}\in\mathcal{P}(\textsc{SCheck})
.

Note that for any given
\sigma
, there may be multiple values of
t,\sigma^{\prime},\mathcal{R}
for which the relation is satisfied. Therefore, the path condition of
\sigma^{\prime}
must be satisfied before assuming that
t
corresponds to an actual value.

Also note that unsatisfiable paths are not pruned during evaluation. These paths may be pruned by checking the satisfiability of
g(\sigma^{\prime})
.

For a list of expressions
\overline{e}
,
\overline{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}
represents a sequence of judgements

\sigma_{0}\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow t_{1}\dashv\sigma_{1},\,,\cdots,\sigma_{n-1}\vdash e_{n}\Downarrow t_{n}\dashv\sigma_{n},\,\mathcal{R}_{n}

where
\sigma_{0}=\sigma
,
e_{1},\cdots,e_{n}=\overline{e}
, and
\mathcal{R}=\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\cdots\cup\mathcal{R}_{n}
.

\frac{}{\sigma\vdash l\Downarrow l\dashv\sigma,\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SEvalLiteral}

\frac{}{\sigma\vdash x\Downarrow\gamma(\sigma)(x)\dashv\sigma,\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SEvalVar}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow t_{1}\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\quad\sigma^{\prime\prime}=\sigma^{\prime}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t_{1}]}{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}\Downarrow t_{1}\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}\quad\textsc{SEvalOrA}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow t_{1}\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}_{1}\quad\sigma^{\prime}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}\mathop{\texttt{!}}t_{1}]\vdash e_{2}\Downarrow t_{2}\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\mathcal{R}_{2}}{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}\Downarrow t_{2}\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2}}\quad\textsc{SEvalOrB}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow t_{1}\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\quad\sigma^{\prime\prime}=\sigma^{\prime}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}\mathop{\texttt{!}}t_{1}]}{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}e_{2}\Downarrow t_{1}\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}\quad\textsc{SEvalAndA}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow t_{1}\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}_{1}\quad\sigma^{\prime}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t_{1}]\vdash e_{2}\Downarrow t_{2}\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\mathcal{R}_{2}}{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}e_{2}\Downarrow t_{2}\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2}}\quad\textsc{SEvalAndB}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow t_{1}\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}_{1}\quad\sigma^{\prime}\vdash e_{2}\Downarrow t_{2}\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\mathcal{R}_{2}}{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\oplus e_{2}\Downarrow t_{1}\oplus t_{2}\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2}}\quad\textsc{SEvalOp}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}{\sigma\vdash\mathop{\texttt{!}}e\Downarrow\mathop{\texttt{!}}t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}\quad\textsc{SEvalNeg}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t_{e}\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\quad g(\sigma^{\prime})\implies t_{e}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}^{\prime}\quad\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime})}{\sigma\vdash e.f\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}\quad\textsc{SEvalField}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t_{e}\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\quad\nexists\,t_{e}^{\prime},t:\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\wedge g(\sigma^{\prime})\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}\quad\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\quad g(\sigma^{\prime})\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}}{\sigma\vdash e.f\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}\quad\textsc{SEvalFieldOptimistic}

\frac{\iota(\sigma)\quad\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t_{e}\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\quad\nexists\,t_{e}^{\prime},t:\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\wedge g(\sigma^{\prime})\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}\quad t=\operatorname{fresh}\quad\sigma^{\prime\prime}=\sigma^{\prime}[\mathcal{H}=\mathcal{H}(\sigma^{\prime});\langle f,\,t_{e},\,t\rangle]}{\sigma\vdash e.f\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\mathcal{R};\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle}\quad\textsc{SEvalFieldImprecise}

\frac{\neg\iota(\sigma)\quad\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t_{e}\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\quad\nexists\,t_{e}^{\prime},t:\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\wedge g(\sigma^{\prime})\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}\quad t=\operatorname{fresh}}{\sigma\vdash e.f\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\{\bot\}}\quad\textsc{SEvalFieldFailure}

C.7.
Deterministic evaluation

We also define separate evaluation semantics for evaluation when branching is unwanted. This is denoted by the judgement

\sigma\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}.

This operation mirrors the behavior of
pc-eval
, and as such, does not modify the symbolic state. Logical operations are not short-circuited as in the previous section; instead, they are formally encoded as conjuncts of a single
SExpr
.

\frac{}{\sigma\vdash l\downarrow l\dashv\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SEvalPCLiteral}

\frac{}{\sigma\vdash x\downarrow\gamma(\sigma)(x)\dashv\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SEvalPCVar}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\downarrow t_{1}\dashv\mathcal{R}_{1}\quad\sigma\vdash e_{2}\downarrow t_{2}\dashv\mathcal{R}_{2}}{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}\downarrow t_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}t_{2}\dashv\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2}}\quad\textsc{SEvalPCOr}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\downarrow t_{1}\dashv\mathcal{R}_{1}\quad\sigma\vdash e_{2}\downarrow t_{2}\dashv\mathcal{R}_{2}}{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}e_{2}\downarrow t_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t_{2}\dashv\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2}}\quad\textsc{SEvalPCAnd}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\downarrow t_{1}\dashv\mathcal{R}_{1}\quad\sigma\vdash e_{2}\downarrow t_{2}\dashv\mathcal{R}_{2}}{\sigma\vdash e_{1}\oplus e_{2}\downarrow t_{1}\oplus t_{2}\dashv\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2}}\quad\textsc{SEvalPCOp}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}}{\sigma\vdash\mathop{\texttt{!}}e\downarrow\mathop{\texttt{!}}t\dashv\mathcal{R}}\quad\textsc{SEvalPCNeg}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}\quad\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)}{\sigma\vdash e.f\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}}\quad\textsc{SEvalPCField}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad\nexists\,t_{e},t:\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\wedge g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}\quad g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}\quad\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathcal{H}(\sigma)}{\sigma\vdash e.f\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}}\quad\textsc{SEvalPCFieldOptimistic}

\frac{\iota(\sigma)\quad\sigma\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad\quad\nexists\,t_{e},t:\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\wedge g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}\quad t=\operatorname{fresh}}{\sigma\vdash e.f\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R};\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle}\quad\textsc{SEvalPCFieldImprecise}

\frac{\neg\iota(\sigma)\quad\sigma\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad\quad\nexists\,t_{e},t:\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\wedge g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}\quad t=\operatorname{fresh}}{\sigma\vdash e.f\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R};\bot}\quad\textsc{SEvalPCFieldMissing}

C.8.
Produce

The
produce
operation adds the information contained in a formula
\tilde{\phi}
to the symbolic state
\sigma
, resulting in a new symbolic state
\sigma^{\prime}
. This is denoted by the judgement

\sigma\vdash\phi\lhd\sigma^{\prime}.

\frac{\sigma[\iota=\top]\vdash\phi\lhd\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\lhd\sigma^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{SProduceImprecise}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\_\quad\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma[g=g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t]}{\sigma\vdash e\lhd\sigma^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{SProduceExpr}

\frac{\overline{\sigma\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\_}\quad\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}(\sigma);\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle]}{\sigma\vdash p(\overline{e})\lhd\sigma^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{SProducePredicate}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\_\quad t=\operatorname{fresh}\quad\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}(\sigma);\langle f,\,t_{e},\,t\rangle]}{\sigma\vdash\texttt{acc}(e.f)\lhd\sigma^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{SProduceField}

\frac{\sigma\vdash\phi_{1}\lhd\sigma^{\prime}\quad\sigma^{\prime}\vdash\phi_{2}\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}}{\sigma\vdash\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}}\quad\textsc{SProduceConjunction}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\_\quad\sigma[g=g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t]\vdash\phi_{1}\lhd\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma\vdash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}\lhd\sigma^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{SProduceIfA}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\_\quad\sigma[g=g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}\mathop{\texttt{!}}t]\vdash\phi_{2}\lhd\sigma^{\prime}}{\sigma\vdash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}\lhd\sigma^{\prime}}\quad\textsc{SProduceIfB}

C.9.
Consume

The
consume
operation checks whether a formula
\tilde{\phi}
is established by the symbolic state, collects runtime checks that are minimally sufficient to establish
\tilde{\phi}
, and removes permissions asserted in
\tilde{\phi}
from the symbolic state. This is denoted by the judgement

\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\Theta

where
\sigma
is the symbolic state containing the currently remaining permissions during consume, and
\sigma_{E}
is the symbolic state containing the original permissions which may used for evaluating expressions.

Note that consume does not branch on operations such as
\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}
that are normally short-circuiting. This is because the evaluation operation
\downarrow
does not modify the path condition but keeps track of logical operators as symbolic values. Values in specifications must be framed anyway (explicitly or inferred), so we must always have the permissions necessary (whether statically or dynamically checked) for evaluating all branches.

\frac{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}[\iota=\top]\vdash\phi\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\Theta}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\rhd\langle\top,\,g(\sigma^{\prime}),\,\gamma(\sigma^{\prime}),\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset\rangle,\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\Theta}\quad\textsc{SConsumeImprecision}

\frac{\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad g(\sigma)\implies t}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash e\rhd\sigma,\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SConsumeValue}

\frac{\iota(\sigma)\quad\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad g(\sigma)\Longarrownot\Longrightarrow t}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash e\rhd\sigma[g=g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t],\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R};t,\,\allowbreak\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SConsumeValueImprecise}

\frac{\neg\iota(\sigma)\quad\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad g(\sigma)\Longarrownot\Longrightarrow t}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash e\rhd\sigma,\,\allowbreak\{\bot\},\,\allowbreak\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SConsumeValueFailure}

\frac{\overline{\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}}\quad\overline{g(\sigma)\implies t\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t^{\prime}}\quad\mathsf{H}(\sigma)=\mathsf{H}^{\prime};\langle p,\,\overline{t^{\prime}}\rangle}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash p(\overline{e})\rhd\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime},\mathcal{H}=\emptyset],\,\allowbreak\bigcup\overline{\mathcal{R}},\,\allowbreak\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\}}\quad\textsc{SConsumePredicate}

\frac{\iota(\sigma)\quad\overline{\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}}\quad\nexists\,\langle p,\,\overline{t^{\prime}}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma):\bigwedge\overline{g(\sigma)\implies t\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t^{\prime}}}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash p(\overline{e})\rhd\sigma[H=\emptyset,\mathcal{H}=\emptyset],\,\allowbreak\bigcup\overline{\mathcal{R}};\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle,\,\allowbreak\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\}}\quad\textsc{SConsumePredicateImprecise}

\frac{\neg\iota(\sigma)\quad\overline{\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}}\quad\nexists\,\langle p,\,\overline{t^{\prime}}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma):\bigwedge\overline{g(\sigma)\implies t\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t^{\prime}}}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash p(\overline{e})\rhd\sigma,\,\allowbreak\{\bot\},\,\allowbreak\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\}}\quad\textsc{SConsumePredicateFailure}

\frac{\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}\quad\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\quad\mathsf{H}^{\prime}=\operatorname{rem_{fp}}(\mathsf{H}(\sigma),\sigma,t_{e},f)\quad\mathcal{H}^{\prime}=\operatorname{rem_{f}}(\mathcal{H}(\sigma),\sigma,t_{e},f)}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{acc}(e.f)\rhd\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime},\mathcal{H}=\mathcal{H}^{\prime}],\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}}\quad\textsc{SConsumeAcc}

\frac{\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}\quad\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\quad\nexists\,t_{e}^{\prime},t:\langle f,\,t_{e},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\wedge(g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e})\quad\mathsf{H}^{\prime}=\operatorname{rem_{f}}(\mathsf{H}(\sigma),\sigma,t_{e},f)\quad\mathcal{H}^{\prime}=\operatorname{rem_{f}}(\mathcal{H}(\sigma),\sigma,t_{e},f)}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{acc}(e.f)\rhd\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime},\mathcal{H}=\mathcal{H}^{\prime}],\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}}\quad\textsc{SConsumeAccOptimistic}

\frac{\iota(\sigma)\quad\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad\nexists\,t_{e}^{\prime},t:\langle f,\,t_{e},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\wedge(g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e})\quad\mathsf{H}^{\prime}=\operatorname{rem_{f}}(\mathsf{H}(\sigma),\sigma,t_{e},f)\quad\mathcal{H}^{\prime}=\operatorname{rem_{f}}(\mathcal{H}(\sigma),\sigma,t_{e},f)}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{acc}(e.f)\rhd\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime},\mathcal{H}=\mathcal{H}^{\prime}],\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R};\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle,\,\allowbreak\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}}\quad\textsc{SConsumeAccImprecise}

\frac{\neg\iota(\sigma)\quad\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad\nexists\,t_{e}^{\prime},t:\langle f,\,t_{e},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\wedge(g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e})}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{acc}(e.f)\rhd\sigma,\,\allowbreak\{\bot\},\,\allowbreak\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}}\quad\textsc{SConsumeAccFailure}

\frac{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\phi_{1}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}_{1},\,\allowbreak\Theta_{1}\quad\sigma^{\prime},\,\sigma_{E}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})]\vdash\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}_{2},\,\allowbreak\Theta_{2}\quad\quad(\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2})\cap\textsc{SPerm}=\emptyset}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2},\,\allowbreak\Theta_{1}\cup\Theta_{2}}\quad\textsc{SConsumeConjunction}

\frac{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\phi_{1}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}_{1},\,\allowbreak\Theta_{1}\quad\sigma^{\prime},\,\sigma_{E}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})]\vdash\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}_{2},\,\allowbreak\Theta_{2}\quad\quad(\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2})\cap\textsc{SPerm}\neq\emptyset}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2};\operatorname{sep}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2}),\,\allowbreak\Theta_{1}\cup\Theta_{2}}\quad\textsc{SConsumeConjunctionImprecise}

\frac{\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad g^{\prime}=g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t\quad\sigma[g=g^{\prime}],\,\sigma_{E}[g=g^{\prime}]\vdash\phi_{1}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\Theta}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\Theta}\quad\textsc{SConsumeConditionalA}

\frac{\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}\quad g^{\prime}=g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}\mathop{\texttt{!}}t\quad\sigma[g=g^{\prime}],\,\sigma_{E}[g=g^{\prime}]\vdash\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\Theta}{\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\Theta}\quad\textsc{SConsumeConditionalB}

The functions
\operatorname{rem_{f}}
,
\operatorname{rem_{fp}}
, and
\operatorname{alias}
are defined as follows:

\displaystyle\operatorname{rem_{f}}(\mathsf{H},\sigma,t,f)

\displaystyle=\{\langle f^{\prime},\,t^{\prime},\,t^{\prime\prime}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}:\neg\operatorname{alias}(\sigma,t,f,t^{\prime},f^{\prime})\}

\displaystyle\operatorname{rem_{fp}}(\mathsf{H},\sigma,t,f)

\displaystyle=\operatorname{rem_{f}}(\mathsf{H},\sigma,t,f)\cup\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}\}

\displaystyle\operatorname{alias}(\sigma,t,f,t^{\prime},f^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}f=f^{\prime}\wedge(g(\sigma)\implies t\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t^{\prime})&\neg\iota(\sigma)\\
(f=f^{\prime})\wedge\operatorname{sat}(g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t^{\prime})&\iota(\sigma)\end{cases}

For ease of notation, the judgement
\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}
applies the rules above, initializing additional parameters and ignoring the parameters that are internal to consume.

\frac{\sigma,\,\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\_}{\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}}\quad\textsc{SConsume}

C.10.
Execute

Symbolic execution is denoted by the small-step judgement

\sigma\vdash s\to s^{\prime}\dashv\sigma^{\prime}.

where
\sigma
is the symbolic state prior to execution,
s
is the statement to execute,
s^{\prime}
is the statement remaining after this execution step and
\sigma^{\prime}
is the symbolic state after the execution step.

Note that as in §
C.6
there may be multiple resulting symbolic states
\sigma^{\prime}
for which the judgement applies, and therefore the path condition of
\sigma^{\prime}
must be satisfied before assuming that
\sigma^{\prime}
corresponds to a particular execution step.

\frac{}{\sigma\vdash\texttt{skip}\texttt{;}~s\to s\dashv\sigma}\quad\textsc{SExecSeq}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\_\quad\gamma^{\prime}=\gamma(\sigma)[x\mapsto t]}{\sigma\vdash x=e\texttt{;}~s\to s\dashv\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}]}\quad\textsc{SExecAssign}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\_\quad\sigma^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{acc}(x.f)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\_\quad\mathsf{H}^{\prime}=\mathsf{H}(\sigma);\langle\gamma(\sigma^{\prime\prime})(x),\,f,\,t\rangle}{\sigma\vdash x.f=e\texttt{;}~s\to s\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime}]}\quad\textsc{SExecAssignField}

\frac{t=\operatorname{fresh}\quad\overline{T~f}=\operatorname{struct}(S)\quad\mathsf{H}^{\prime}=\mathsf{H}(\sigma);\overline{\langle f,\,t,\,\operatorname{default}(T)\rangle}}{\sigma\vdash x=\texttt{alloc}(S)\texttt{;}~s\to s\dashv\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime}]}\quad\textsc{SExecAlloc}

\frac{\overline{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t_{e}\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\_}\quad\overline{x}=\operatorname{params}(m)\quad\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=[\overline{x\mapsto t_{e}}]]\vdash\operatorname{pre}(m)\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\_\quad t=\operatorname{fresh}\quad\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=[\overline{x\mapsto t_{e}},\texttt{result}\mapsto t]]\vdash\operatorname{post}(m)\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}}{\sigma\vdash y\mathbin{\texttt{=}}m(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\to s\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)[y\mapsto t]]}\quad\textsc{SExecCall}

\frac{\sigma\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\_\quad\sigma^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}}{\sigma\vdash\texttt{assert}~\phi\texttt{;}~s\to s\dashv\sigma[g=g(\sigma^{\prime\prime})]}\quad\textsc{SExecAssert}

\frac{\overline{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\_}\quad\overline{x}=\operatorname{predicate\_params}(p)\quad\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=[\overline{x\mapsto t}]]\vdash\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\_\quad\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}=\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma),\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime\prime});\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle]}{\sigma\vdash\texttt{fold}~p(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\to s\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\quad\textsc{SExecFold}

\frac{\overline{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\_}\quad\sigma^{\prime}\vdash p(\overline{e})\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\_\quad\overline{x}=\operatorname{predicate\_params}(p)\quad\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=[\overline{x\mapsto t}]]\vdash\operatorname{predicate}(p)\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}}{\sigma\vdash\texttt{unfold}~p(e_{1},\cdots,e_{n})\texttt{;}~s\to s\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)]}\quad\textsc{SExecUnfold}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\_}{\sigma\vdash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~s_{1}~\texttt{else}~s_{2}\texttt{;}~s\to s_{1}\texttt{;}~s\dashv\sigma^{\prime}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t]}\quad\textsc{SExecIfA}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\_}{\sigma\vdash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~s_{1}~\texttt{else}~s_{2}\texttt{;}~s\to s_{2}\texttt{;}~s\dashv\sigma^{\prime}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}\mathop{\texttt{!}}t]}\quad\textsc{SExecIfB}

\frac{\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\_\quad\overline{x}=\operatorname{modified}(s)\quad\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma^{\prime})[\overline{x\mapsto\operatorname{fresh}}]]\vdash\tilde{\phi}\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}\quad\sigma^{\prime\prime}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\_\quad}{\sigma\vdash\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime}\to s^{\prime}\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime\prime})\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}\mathop{\texttt{!}}t]}\quad\textsc{SExecWhileSkip}

Definition 0.

The helper function
\operatorname{rem}(\sigma,\tilde{\phi})
returns the set of all permissions remaining in
\sigma
if
\tilde{\phi}
is not completely precise:

\operatorname{rem}(\sigma,\tilde{\phi}):=\begin{cases}\emptyset&\text{if $\tilde{\phi}$ is completely precise}\\
\{\langle t,\,f\rangle:\langle f,\,t,\,t^{\prime}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\}~\cup\\
\quad\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle:\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\}&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

This is used to symbolically calculate the required exclusion frame.

C.11.
Verification states

Definition 0.

A
verification state

\Sigma
is either an abstract symbol or a triple consisting of a symbolic state, a statement, and a post-condition.

\Sigma::=\mathsf{init}\quad|\quad\mathsf{final}\quad|\quad\langle\sigma,\,s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle

The reachability of verification transitions, as determined by modular verification for a given program
\Pi
, is determined by the judgement

\Pi\vdash\Sigma\to\Sigma^{\prime}

where
\Sigma
is the beginning verification state and
\Sigma^{\prime}
is the next verification state.

\frac{}{\langle s,\,M,\,P,\,S\rangle\vdash\mathsf{init}\to\langle\langle\bot,\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,\texttt{true}\rangle,\,s,\,\texttt{true}\rangle}\quad\textsc{SVerifyInit}

\frac{m\in M\quad\overline{x}=\operatorname{params}(m)\quad\langle\bot,\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,[\overline{x\mapsto\operatorname{fresh}}],\,\texttt{true}\rangle\vdash\operatorname{pre}(m)\lhd\sigma}{\langle s,\,M,\,P,\,S\rangle\vdash\mathsf{init}\to\langle\sigma,\,\operatorname{body}(m)\texttt{;}~\texttt{skip},\,\operatorname{post}(m)\rangle}\quad\textsc{SVerifyMethod}

\frac{\Pi\vdash\_\to\langle\sigma_{0},\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime},\,\tilde{\phi}_{0}\rangle\quad\overline{x}=\operatorname{modified}(s)\quad\langle\bot,\,\gamma(\sigma_{0})[\overline{x\mapsto\operatorname{fresh}}],\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,g(\sigma_{0})\rangle\vdash\tilde{\phi}\lhd\sigma_{0}^{\prime}\quad\sigma_{0}^{\prime}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}}{\Pi\vdash\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime},\,\tilde{\phi}_{0}\rangle\to\langle\sigma_{0}^{\prime}[g=g(\sigma_{0}^{\prime})\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t],\,s\texttt{;}~\texttt{skip},\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle}\quad\textsc{SVerifyLoopBody}

\frac{\Pi\vdash\_\to\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}^{\prime}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime},\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\quad\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}^{\prime}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\_\quad\overline{x}=\operatorname{modified}(s)\quad\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)[\overline{x\mapsto\operatorname{fresh}}]]\vdash\tilde{\phi}^{\prime}\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}}{\Pi\vdash\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}^{\prime}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime},\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\to\\
\\
\hskip 30.00005pt\langle\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}^{\prime}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime},\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle}\quad\textsc{SVerifyLoop}

\frac{\Pi\vdash\_\to\langle\sigma,\,s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\quad\sigma\vdash s\to s^{\prime}\dashv\sigma^{\prime}}{\Pi\vdash\langle\sigma,\,s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\to\langle\sigma^{\prime},\,s^{\prime},\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle}\quad\textsc{SVerifyStep}

\frac{\Pi\vdash\_\to\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{skip},\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\quad\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\_}{\Pi\vdash\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{skip},\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\to\mathsf{final}}\quad\textsc{SVerifyFinal}

Definition 0.

A verification state
\Sigma
is
reachable
from program
\Pi
if
\Sigma=\mathsf{init}
or
\Pi\vdash\_\to\Sigma,\_,\_,\_
.

A verification state
\Sigma
is
reachable
from program
\Pi

with valuation

V
if
\Sigma
is reachable from
\Pi
and
V
is defined for all symbolic values contained in
\Sigma
.

C.12.
Guards

A guard judgement determines the set of runtime checks that must be satisfied at the given verification state before taking the next execution step, and determines the exclusion frame required to preserve the validity of heap chunks contained by the current symbolic state. This is denoted by the judgement

\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime},\mathcal{R},\Theta

where
\Sigma
is the current verification state,
\sigma^{\prime}
is the intermediate state,
\mathcal{R}
is the set of required checks, and
\Theta
is the exclusion frame (represented as a set of symbolic permissions).

\frac{}{\mathsf{init}\rightharpoonup\langle\bot,\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,\texttt{true}\rangle\dashv\emptyset,\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SGuardInit}

\frac{}{\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{skip}\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma\dashv\emptyset,\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SGuardSeq}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow\_\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}{\langle\sigma,\,x=e\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SGuardAssign}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow\_\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\quad\sigma^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{acc}(x.f)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime}}{\langle\sigma,\,x.f=e\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime},\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SGuardAssignField}

\frac{}{\langle\sigma,\,x=\texttt{alloc}(S)\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma\dashv\emptyset,\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SGuardAlloc}

\frac{\overline{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}\quad\overline{x}=\operatorname{params}(m)\quad\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=[\overline{x\mapsto t}]]\vdash\operatorname{pre}(m)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}}{\langle\sigma,\,y\mathbin{\texttt{=}}m(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)]\dashv\overline{\mathcal{R}}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime},\,\operatorname{rem}(\sigma^{\prime\prime},\operatorname{pre}(m))}\quad\textsc{SGuardCall}

\frac{\sigma\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}}{\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{assert}~\phi\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SGuardAssert}

\frac{\overline{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}\quad\overline{x}=\operatorname{predicate\_params}(p)\quad\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=[\overline{x\mapsto t}]]\vdash\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}}{\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{fold}~p(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)]\dashv\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\cup\bigcup\overline{\mathcal{R}},\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SGuardFold}

\frac{\overline{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}\quad\sigma^{\prime}\vdash p(\overline{e})\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}}{\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{unfold}~p(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\cup\bigcup\overline{\mathcal{R}},\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SGuardUnfold}

\frac{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow\_\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}{\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~s_{1}~\texttt{else}~s_{2}\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SGuardIf}

\frac{\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\quad\overline{x}=\operatorname{modified}(s)\quad\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma^{\prime})[\overline{x\mapsto\operatorname{fresh}}]]\vdash\tilde{\phi}\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}\quad\sigma^{\prime\prime}\vdash e\downarrow\_\dashv\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime}}{\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime},\,\tilde{\phi}^{\prime}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime\prime})]\dashv\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime},\,\operatorname{rem}(\sigma^{\prime},\tilde{\phi})}\quad\textsc{SGuardWhile}

\frac{\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}}{\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{skip},\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\emptyset}\quad\textsc{SGuardFinish}

C.13.
Valid states

Definition 0.

A verification state
\Sigma

corresponds
with valuation
V
to an execution state
\Gamma
if
\Sigma=\Gamma
, or
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle
for some
H,\alpha,\rho,s
and
\Sigma=\langle\sigma,\,s,\,\_\rangle
for some
\sigma
, such that
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma
.

Definition 0.

A
partial state

is

\Sigma
with valuation
V
if one of the following cases apply:

Part 0.1.

\mathcal{S}=\mathsf{nil}

Part 0.2.

\mathcal{S}=\langle\rho,\,\alpha,\,y\mathbin{\texttt{=}}m(e_{1},\cdots,e_{k})\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}
for some
\rho
,

,

,

,

,

,

,

,

and
\sigma^{\prime}
such that:

(5)

\displaystyle\text{The partial state $\langle H,\,\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle$ is validated by $\Sigma^{\prime}$ and $V^{\prime}$},

(6)

\displaystyle\Sigma^{\prime}\text{ is reachable from $\Pi$ with valuation $V^{\prime}$},\quad s(\Sigma^{\prime})=s(\mathcal{S}),

(7)

\displaystyle x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}=\operatorname{params}(m),

(8)

\displaystyle\sigma_{0}=\sigma(\Sigma^{\prime}),\quad\sigma_{0}\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow t_{1}\dashv\sigma_{1},\,\_,\quad\cdots,\quad\sigma_{k-1}\vdash e_{k}\Downarrow t_{k}\dashv\sigma_{k},\,\_,

(9)

\displaystyle\forall\,1\leq i\leq k:V(\gamma(\Sigma)(x_{i}))=V^{\prime}(t_{i}),

(10)

\displaystyle\sigma_{k}\vdash\operatorname{pre}(m)\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\_,\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma_{0})],\quad\text{and}

(11)

\displaystyle\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)=\operatorname{post}(m)

Part 0.3.

\mathcal{S}=\langle\rho,\,\alpha,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime}\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}
for some
\rho
,

,

,

,

,

,

(14)

\displaystyle\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\_,\quad\text{and}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)=\tilde{\phi}

Definition 0.

For a program
\Pi
, a dynamic state
\Gamma
is

by

and valuation

Part 0.2.

is validated by
\Sigma
and
V
.

Definition 0.

\Gamma
is a

if
\Gamma
is validated by some
\Sigma
.

Appendix D
Soundness

This section contains the proof of soundness, culminating in theorems
55
,
56
, and
69
.

D.1.
Cross-cutting lemmas

Lemma 0 (Relating expression framing and exact footprints).

\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha
if and only if
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
.

Proof.

By induction on the syntax forms of
e
:

Case 1.

l
: Then
\llfloor l\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}=\emptyset
, thus
\llfloor l\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha
, and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}l
by
FrameLiteral
.

Case 2.

v
: Then
\llfloor v\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}=\emptyset
, thus
\llfloor v\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha
, and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}v
by
FrameVar
.

Case 3.

e.f
:

Suppose that
\llfloor e.f\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha
. Then by definition
\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha
and
\langle\ell,\,f\rangle\in\alpha
for some
\ell
such that
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\ell
. By induction
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
, by
AssertAcc

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{acc}(e.f)
, and thus by
FrameField
,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e.f
.

Suppose that
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e.f
. Then by
FrameField
,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{acc}(e.f)
, thus by induction
\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,env\rangle}\subseteq\alpha
and by
AssertAcc
,
\langle\ell,\,f\rangle\in\alpha
for some
\ell
such that
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\ell
. Thus
\llfloor e.f\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}=\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle};\langle\ell,\,f\rangle\subseteq\alpha
.

Case 4.

e_{1}\oplus e_{2}
:

\displaystyle\llfloor e_{1}\oplus e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha

\displaystyle\iff\llfloor e_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha~\text{and}~\llfloor e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha

by definition

\displaystyle\iff\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}~\text{and}~\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{2}

by induction

\displaystyle\iff\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\oplus e_{2}

by
FrameOp

Case 5.

e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}
:

\displaystyle\llfloor e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha\implies

\displaystyle~\textbf{either}~\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{true}~\text{and}~\llfloor e_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha

by definition

\displaystyle\quad\implies\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{true}~\text{and}~\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}

by induction

\displaystyle\quad\implies\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}

by
FrameOrA

\displaystyle~\textbf{or}~\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{false}~\text{and}~\llfloor e_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\cup\llfloor e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha

by definition

\displaystyle\quad\implies\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{false},~\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1},

\displaystyle\hskip 35.00005pt\text{and}~\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{2}

by induction

\displaystyle\quad\implies\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}

by
FrameOrB

\displaystyle\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}\implies

\displaystyle~\textbf{either}~\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{true}~\text{and}~\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1}

by
FrameOrA

\displaystyle\quad\implies\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{true}~\text{and}~\llfloor e_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha

by induction

\displaystyle\quad\implies\llfloor e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha

by definition

\displaystyle~\textbf{or}~\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{false},~\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{1},

\displaystyle\hskip 22.50003pt\text{and}~\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e_{2}

by
FrameOrB

\displaystyle\quad\implies\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow\texttt{false}~\text{and}~\llfloor e_{1}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\cup\llfloor e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha

by induction

\displaystyle\quad\implies\llfloor e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{||}}e_{2}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha

by definition

Case 6.

e_{1}\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}e_{2}
: Similar to case
D.1
.

Case 7.

\mathop{\texttt{!}}e
:

\displaystyle\llfloor\mathop{\texttt{!}}e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha

\displaystyle\iff\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha

by definition

\displaystyle\iff\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e

by induction

\displaystyle\iff\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}\mathop{\texttt{!}}e

by
FrameNeg

∎

Lemma 0 (Relating formula assertion/framing and exact footprints).

If
\langle H,\,\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}
,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\tilde{\phi}
, and
\alpha^{\prime}\subseteq\alpha
, then
\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha
.

Proof.

By induction on
\langle H,\,\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}
:

Case 1.

AssertImprecise
–
\langle H,\,\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{?}*\phi
:

By
AssertImprecise
,
\langle H,\,\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi
and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\phi
, therefore by induction
