\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma
,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,[\overline{x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t^{\prime})}]\rangle\vDash\operatorname{predicate}(p)
where
\overline{x}=\operatorname{predicate\_params}(p)
. Thus by
AssertPredicate

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash p(\overline{e})
, which proves (
17
). Also,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash p(\overline{e})
by lemma
9
.

Let
\alpha^{\prime}=V\llparenthesis\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\rrparenthesis_{H}
, therefore
\alpha^{\prime}=\llfloor\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,[\overline{x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t)}]\rangle}=\llfloor\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,[\overline{x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t^{\prime})}]\rangle}
.

By
SConsumePredicate

\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime},\mathcal{H}=\emptyset]
where
\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime};\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle
. Thus
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime})
since
\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime})\subset\mathsf{H}(\sigma)
.

Then
V^{\prime}\llparenthesis h\rrparenthesis_{H}\cap\alpha^{\prime}=\emptyset
for all
h\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime})
since
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}(\sigma)
,
\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)
, and
\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\notin\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime})
. Therefore, by lemma
16
,
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\alpha^{\prime}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime})
.

Also, since
\mathcal{H}(\sigma^{\prime})=\emptyset
,
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\alpha^{\prime}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathcal{H}(\sigma^{\prime})
.

Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
since
\sigma^{\prime}
and
\sigma
differ only in their
\mathsf{H}
and
\mathcal{H}
components.

By lemmas
23

\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
for each
\mathcal{R}
, thus
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
for each
e
by lemma
30
, thus
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}p(\overline{e})
by
IFramePredicate
. Thus (
16
) holds.

Case 6.

SConsumePredicateImprecise
–
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash p(\overline{e})\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\bigcup\overline{\mathcal{R}};\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle,\,\allowbreak\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\}
:

By
SConsumePredicateImprecise
, for each
e
,
\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}
for some
t
and
\mathcal{R}
. Let
V^{\prime}
be corresponding valuation for this case, thus
V^{\prime}
extends the respective individual corresponding valuations and for each
\mathcal{R}
,
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
by lemma
23

Therefore, for each
e
and corresponding
t
,
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t)
by lemma
30
. By

SConsumePredicateImprecise
, for each
t
,
g(\sigma)\implies t\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t^{\prime}
for some
t^{\prime}
, thus
V^{\prime}(t)=V^{\prime}(t^{\prime})
since
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma))=\texttt{true}
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{i}\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t_{i}^{\prime})
.

Also,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\}
by assumptions and lemma
23
. Thus
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,[\overline{x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t)}]\rangle\vDash\operatorname{predicate}(p)
by
CheckPred
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash p(\overline{e})
by
AssertPredicate
.

By
SConsumePredicateImprecise

\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\emptyset,\mathcal{H}=\emptyset]
, thus
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\}\rrparenthesis_{H}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime})
and
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\}\rrparenthesis_{H}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathcal{H}(\sigma^{\prime})
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V\llparenthesis\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\}\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
since
\sigma^{\prime}
and
\sigma
differ only in their
\mathsf{H}
and
\mathcal{H}
components.

For each
e
,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
by lemma
30
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}p(\overline{e})
by
IFramePredicate
, which completes the proof of (
16
).

Also,
\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\in\textsc{SPerm}
is in the resulting set of checks, which contradicts the premises of (
17
). Therefore it is vacuously true.

Case 7.

SConsumePredicateFailure
–
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash p(\overline{e})\rhd\sigma,\,\allowbreak\{\bot\},\,\allowbreak\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\}
:

\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\{\bot\}
is a contradiction, thus the lemma vacuously holds.

Case 8.

SConsumeAcc
–
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{acc}(e.f)\rhd\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime},\mathcal{H}=\mathcal{H}^{\prime}],\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}
:

By
SConsumeAcc

\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\mathcal{R}
. Let
V^{\prime}
be the corresponding valuation, thus
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation for this case.

Thus
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t_{e})
by lemma
30
. Also
g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}
by
SConsumeAcc
, thus
V^{\prime}(t_{e}^{\prime})=V^{\prime}(t_{e})
since
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma))=\texttt{true}
. Therefore,
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t_{e}^{\prime})
.

Since
\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)
by
SConsumeAcc
and
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}(\sigma)
,
\langle V^{\prime}(t_{e}^{\prime}),\,f\rangle\in\alpha
. Thus
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{acc}(e.f)
by
AssertAcc
, which proves (
17
). Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{acc}(e.f)
by lemma
9
since
\alpha\subseteq\alpha_{E}
.

Let
\mathsf{H}^{\prime}=\operatorname{rem_{fp}}(\mathsf{H}(\sigma),\sigma,t_{e},f)
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\{\langle V^{\prime}(t_{e}),\,f\rangle\}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}^{\prime}
by lemma
45
. Also,
\{\langle V^{\prime}(t_{e}^{\prime}),\,f\rangle\}=\{\langle V^{\prime}(t_{e}),\,f\rangle\}=V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}\rrparenthesis_{H}
. Thus
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}\rrparenthesis_{H}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}^{\prime}
.

Likewise, let
\mathcal{H}^{\prime}=\operatorname{rem_{f}}(\mathcal{H}(\sigma),\sigma,t_{e},f)
. Then similarly
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V\llparenthesis\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}\rrparenthesis_{H}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathcal{H}^{\prime}
by lemma
46
.

By
SConsumeAcc
,
\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime},\mathcal{H}=\mathcal{H}^{\prime}]
. Now, since
\sigma
and
\sigma^{\prime}
differ only in their
\mathsf{H}
and
\mathcal{H}
components, using the properties of
\mathsf{H}^{\prime}
and
\mathcal{H}^{\prime}
shown above,
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V\llparenthesis\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
.

Finally,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
by lemma
30
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\texttt{acc}(e.f)
by
IFrameAcc
, which completes the proof of (
16
).

Case 9.

SConsumeAccOptimistic
–
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{acc}(e.f)\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}
:
Similar to case
D.5
, except to show that
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\{\langle V^{\prime}(t_{e}^{\prime}),\,f\rangle\}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}^{\prime}
.

Since
\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathcal{H}(\sigma)
,
\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\neq\emptyset
. Therefore, since
\sigma
is well-formed,
\iota(\sigma)=\top
. Let
\mathsf{H}^{\prime}=\operatorname{rem_{f}}(\mathsf{H}(\sigma),\sigma,t_{e}^{\prime},f)
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\{\langle V^{\prime}(t_{e}^{\prime}),\,f\rangle\}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}^{\prime}
by lemma
42
.

Continue as in case
D.5
.

Case 10.

SConsumeAccImprecise
–
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{acc}(e.f)\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R};\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle,\,\allowbreak\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}
:

By
SConsumeAcc

\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\mathcal{R}
. Let
V^{\prime}
be the corresponding valuation, thus
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation for this case.

Then
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
by assumptions and lemma
23
. Thus
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t_{e})
by lemma
30
.

Also,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle
by assumptions and lemma
23
. Then
\langle V^{\prime}(t_{e}),\,f\rangle\in\alpha_{E}
by
CheckAcc
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{acc}(e.f)
by
AssertAcc
.

Let
\mathsf{H}^{\prime}=\operatorname{rem_{f}}(\mathsf{H}(\sigma),\sigma,t_{e},f)
. By
SConsumeAccImprecise

\iota(\sigma)
. Thus
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\{\langle V^{\prime}(t_{e}),\,f\rangle\}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}^{\prime}
by lemma
42
. Also,
\{\langle V^{\prime}(t_{e}),\,f\rangle\}=V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}\rrparenthesis_{H}
. Thus
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}\rrparenthesis_{H}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}^{\prime}
.

Likewise, let
\mathcal{H}^{\prime}=\operatorname{rem_{f}}(\mathcal{H}(\sigma),\sigma,t_{e},f)
. Then
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V\llparenthesis\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\rrparenthesis_{H}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathcal{H}^{\prime}
by lemma
46
.

By
SConsumeAcc
,
\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma[\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime},\mathcal{H}=\mathcal{H}^{\prime}]
. Now, since
\sigma
and
\sigma^{\prime}
differ only in their
\mathsf{H}
and
\mathcal{H}
components, using the properties of
\mathsf{H}^{\prime}
and
\mathcal{H}^{\prime}
shown above,
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V\llparenthesis\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
.

By lemma
30

\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
, therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\texttt{acc}(e.f)\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}
by
IFrameAcc
. Thus (
16
) holds.

Also,
\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\in\textsc{SPerm}
is in the resulting set of checks, which contradicts the premises of (
17
), therefore it vacuously holds.

Case 11.

SConsumeAccFailure
–
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{acc}(e.f)\rhd\sigma,\,\allowbreak\{\bot\},\,\allowbreak\{\langle t_{e},\,f\rangle\}
:

\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\{\bot\}
is a contradiction, thus the lemma vacuously holds.

Case 12.

SConsumeConjunction
–
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2},\,\allowbreak\Theta_{1}\cup\Theta_{2}
:

By
SConsumeConjunction

\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\phi_{1}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}_{1},\,\allowbreak\Theta_{1}
and
\sigma^{\prime},\,\sigma_{E}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})]\vdash\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}_{2},\,\allowbreak\Theta_{2}
. Let
V_{1}
and
V^{\prime}
be the respective corresponding valuations, with initial valuations
V
and
V_{1}
, respectively. Then
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation for this case.

By lemma
40
,
g(\sigma^{\prime\prime})\implies g(\sigma^{\prime})
. Thus
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=V_{1}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
. Also,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V_{1}}\mathcal{R}_{1}
by lemma
23
, since
V^{\prime}
extends
V_{1}
.

By
SConsumeConjunction

(\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2})\cap\textsc{SPerm}=\emptyset
, thus
\mathcal{R}_{1}\cap\textsc{SPerm}=\emptyset
.

Let
\alpha_{1}=V\llparenthesis\Theta_{1}\rrparenthesis_{H}
. By induction, using (
17
),
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}
. Thus
\langle H,\,\alpha_{1},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}
and
\alpha_{1}\subseteq\alpha
by lemma
47
.

Also
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\alpha_{1},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{14.34724pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-14.34724pt\rule[0.75348pt]{14.34724pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-14.34724pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[14.34724pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V_{1}}$}}}\hskip-14.34724pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[14.34724pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
by induction,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{14.34724pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-14.34724pt\rule[0.75348pt]{14.34724pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-14.34724pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[14.34724pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V_{1}}$}}}\hskip-14.34724pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[14.34724pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma_{E}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})]
since
V_{1}
extends
V
and
V_{1}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
, and
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V}\mathcal{R}_{2}
by lemma
23
. Finally, by assumptions
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime\prime}))=\texttt{true}
, and
(\alpha\setminus\alpha_{1})\subseteq\alpha\subseteq\alpha_{E}
. Thus by induction
\langle H,\,(\alpha\setminus\alpha_{1})\setminus V\llparenthesis\Theta_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime\prime}
.

Also by induction, using (
17
),
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\alpha_{1},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{2}
.

Now
(\alpha\setminus\alpha_{1})\subseteq\alpha
,
\alpha_{1}\subseteq\alpha
, and
(\alpha\setminus\alpha_{1})\cap\alpha_{1}=\emptyset
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}
by
AssertConjunction
, which proves (
17
). Then by lemma
9

\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}
.

As shown before,
\langle H,\,(\alpha\setminus\alpha_{1})\setminus V\llparenthesis\Theta_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime\prime}
, and
(\alpha\setminus\alpha_{1})\setminus V\llparenthesis\Theta_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H}=\alpha\setminus(V\llparenthesis\Theta_{1}\rrparenthesis_{H}\cup V\llparenthesis\Theta_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H})=\alpha\setminus V\llparenthesis\Theta_{1}\cup\Theta_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H}
, therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V\llparenthesis\Theta_{1}\cup\Theta_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime\prime}
.

By induction
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\phi_{1}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\phi_{2}
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}
by
IFrameConjunction
, which completes the proof of (
16
).

Case 13.

SConsumeConjunctionImprecise
–
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2};\operatorname{sep}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2}),\,\allowbreak\Theta_{1}\cup\Theta_{2}
:

Similar to case
D.5
, except when showing that
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}
and when proving (
17
):

By induction
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{2}
. Thus by lemma
47

\langle H,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta_{1}\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}
,
\langle H,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{2}
, and
V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta_{1}\rrparenthesis_{H}\cup V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H}\subseteq\alpha_{E}
.

By assumptions
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\operatorname{sep}(\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2})
. Then by
CheckSep

V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta_{1}\rrparenthesis_{H}\cap V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H}=\emptyset
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}
by
AssertConjunction
.

By
SConsumeConjunctionImprecise

(\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2})\cap\textsc{SPerm}\neq\emptyset
. Therefore the premises of (
17
) do not hold, therefore it is vacuously true.

Case 14.

SConsumeConditionalA
–
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\Theta
:

By
SConsumeConditionalA
,
\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}
and
\sigma[g=g^{\prime}],\,\sigma_{E}[g=g^{\prime}]\vdash\phi_{1}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\Theta
where
g^{\prime}=g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t
. Let
V_{1}
and
V^{\prime}
be the respective corresponding valuations, with initial valuations
V
and
V_{1}
, respectively. Then
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation for this case.

By lemma
40

g(\sigma^{\prime})\implies g^{\prime}=g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t
, thus
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t)=V_{1}(g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t)=\texttt{true}
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{14.34724pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-14.34724pt\rule[0.75348pt]{14.34724pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-14.34724pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[14.34724pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V_{1}}$}}}\hskip-14.34724pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[14.34724pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma[g=g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t]
and
\langle\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t,\,H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{14.34724pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-14.34724pt\rule[0.75348pt]{14.34724pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-14.34724pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[14.34724pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V_{1}}$}}}\hskip-14.34724pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[14.34724pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma_{E}[g=g(\sigma)]{\rho}
.

By assumptions and lemma
23

\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V_{1}}\mathcal{R}
. Thus
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow V_{1}(t)
by lemma
30
. Furthermore,
V_{1}(t)=\texttt{true}
since
g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t\implies t
, thus
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{true}
. Finally,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi_{1}
by induction. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}
by
AssertIfA
.

Also by induction
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
.

Finally,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}\phi_{1}
by induction, and
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
by lemmas
30
. As shown before,
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{true}
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}
by
FrameIfA
, which completes the proof of (
16
).

Now suppose that
(\mathcal{R}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime})\cap\textsc{SPerm}=\emptyset
, thus
\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\cap\textsc{SPerm}=\emptyset
. Then by induction
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi
, and as before
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{true}
, therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}
by
AssertIfA
, which completes the proof of (
17
).

Case 15.

SConsumeConditionalB
–
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\Theta
: Similar to case
D.5
.

∎

Lemma 0 (Soundness of consume (long form)).

Let
\tilde{\phi}
be some specification,
\sigma
and
\sigma_{E}
some well-formed symbolic states such that
g(\sigma)\implies g(\sigma_{E})
, and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle
some evaluation state such that
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma
and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma_{E}
.

If
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\Theta
with corresponding valuation
V^{\prime}
,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
, and
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
, then

\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}\quad\text{and}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}.

Proof.

Suppose that
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\Theta
with corresponding valuation
V^{\prime}
,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E}\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
, and
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
. Then one of the following cases applies

Case 1.

\tilde{\phi}
is imprecise, i.e.
\tilde{\phi}=\texttt{?}*\phi
for some
\phi\in\textsc{Formula}
:

Then, since
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\Theta
, by
SConsumeImprecision

\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}[\iota=\top]\vdash\phi\rhd\sigma_{0},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\Theta
for some
\sigma_{0}
where
\sigma^{\prime}=\langle\top,\,g(\sigma_{0}),\,\gamma(\sigma_{0}),\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset\rangle
. Let
V^{\prime}
be the corresponding valuation, therefore
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation for the original derivation.

Thus by lemma
48
,
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi
,
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma_{0}
, and
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmI}}\phi
.

Then
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frmE}}\phi
by lemma
3
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi
.

Also,
\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime})=\mathcal{H}(\sigma^{\prime})=\emptyset
, thus
\langle H,\,\emptyset\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime})
and
\langle H,\,\emptyset\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathcal{H}(\sigma^{\prime})
. Since
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma_{0}
and
\gamma(\sigma^{\prime})=\gamma(\sigma_{0})
and
g(\sigma^{\prime})=g(\sigma_{0})
,
\rho\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\gamma(\sigma^{\prime})
and
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\emptyset,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
, and thus
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\texttt{?}*\phi\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
by lemma
9
.

Case 2.

\tilde{\phi}
is precise, i.e.
\tilde{\phi}=\phi
:

Then by lemma
48

\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha_{E},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi
.

By lemma
47

\langle H,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi
. Then by lemma
4

\llfloor\phi\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle}\subseteq V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H}
, since
\phi
is a specification. Therefore
\alpha\setminus V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H}\subseteq\alpha\setminus\llfloor\phi\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle}
, thus
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\phi\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
.

∎

Lemma 0 (Soundness of consume (short form)).

Let
\tilde{\phi}
be some specification,
\sigma
be some well-formed symbolic state,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle
some evaluation state, and
V
be some valuation such that

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma
.

If
\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}
with corresponding valuation
V^{\prime}
,
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
, and
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
then

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}\quad\text{and}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}.

Proof.

Suppose
\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}
with corresponding valuation
V^{\prime}
,
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
, and
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
. Then
\sigma,\,\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\mathcal{R},\,\allowbreak\_
by
SConsume
. Let
V^{\prime}
be the corresponding valuation, thus
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation for the original derivation.

Trivially
g(\sigma)\implies g(\sigma)
, thus the conditions of lemma
49
are satisfied, and thus
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
.
∎

Lemma 0 (Progress of consume (long form)).

For any heap
H
,
\sigma
,
\sigma_{E}
,
\tilde{\phi}
, and valuation
V
, if
V(g(\sigma_{E}))=\texttt{true}
then
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
such that
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
where
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation.

Proof.

By induction on the syntax forms of
\phi
:

Case 1.

e\in\textsc{Expr}
:
By lemma
32
,
\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\_
for some
t
. Then one of the following cases applies to yield
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash e\rhd\sigma,\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
:

Case 1(a).

g(\sigma)\implies t
: Then
SConsumeValue
applies.

Case 1(b).

\iota(\sigma)
and
g(\sigma)\Longarrownot\Longrightarrow t
: Then
SConsumeValueImprecise
applies.

Case 1(c).

\neg\iota(\sigma)
and
g(\sigma)\Longarrownot\Longrightarrow t
: Then
SConsumeValueFailure
applies.

Case 2.

p(\overline{e})
–
p\in\textsc{Predicate},\overline{e\in\textsc{Expr}}
:

By lemma
32
, for each
e
,
\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\_
for some
t
. Then one of the following cases applies to yield
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash p(\overline{e})\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
where
g(\sigma^{\prime})=g(\sigma)
:

Case 2(a).

p(\overline{t^{\prime}})\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)
and
g(\sigma)\implies\overline{t\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t^{\prime}}
for some
\overline{t^{\prime}}
: Then
SConsumePredicate
applies.

Case 2(b).

\iota(\sigma)
and
\nexists\,\langle p,\,\overline{t^{\prime}}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma):\bigwedge\overline{g(\sigma)\implies t\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t^{\prime}}
: Then
SConsumePredicateImprecise
applies.

Case 2(c).

\neg\iota(\sigma)
and
\nexists\,\langle p,\,\overline{t^{\prime}}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma):\bigwedge\overline{g(\sigma)\implies t\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t^{\prime}}
: Then
SConsumePredicateFailure
applies.

Case 3.

\texttt{acc}(e.f)
–
e\in\textsc{Expr},f\in\textsc{Field}
:

By lemma
32
,
\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t_{e}\dashv\_
for some
t_{e}
. Note that
\operatorname{rem_{f}}
and
\operatorname{rem_{fp}}
are defined for all inputs. Then one of the following cases applies to yield
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{acc}(e.f)\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
where
g(\sigma^{\prime})=g(\sigma^{\prime})
:

Case 3(a).

\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)
and
g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}
for some
t_{e}^{\prime}
and
t
: Then
SConsumeAcc
applies.

Case 3(b).

\nexists\,t_{e}^{\prime},t:\langle f,\,t_{e},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\wedge(g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e})
and
\langle f,\,t_{e}^{\prime},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)
for some
t_{e}^{\prime}
and
t
where
g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e}
: Then

SConsumeAccOptimistic
applies.

Case 3(c).

\nexists\,t_{e}^{\prime},t:\langle f,\,t_{e},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\wedge(g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e})
and
\iota(\sigma)
: Then

SConsumeAccImprecise
applies.

Case 3(d).

\nexists\,t_{e}^{\prime},t:\langle f,\,t_{e},\,t\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\wedge(g(\sigma)\implies t_{e}^{\prime}\mathbin{\texttt{==}}t_{e})
and
\neg\iota(\sigma)
: Then

SConsumeAccFailure
applies.

Case 4.

\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}
–
\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\in\textsc{Formula}

By induction,
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\phi_{1}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
such that
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
where
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation.

Then also by induction,
\sigma^{\prime},\,\sigma_{E}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})]\vdash\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
for some
\sigma^{\prime\prime}
such that
V^{\prime\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime\prime}))=\texttt{true}
where
V^{\prime\prime}
is the corresponding valuation, with initial valuation
V^{\prime}
. Then one of the following cases applies to yield
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\phi_{1}*\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
:

Case 4(a).

(\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2})\cap\textsc{SPerm}\neq\emptyset
: Then
SConsumeConjunctionImprecise
applies.

Case 4(b).

(\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2})\cap\textsc{SPerm}=\emptyset
: Then
SConsumeConjunction
applies.

Case 5.

\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}
–
e\in\textsc{Expr},\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\in\textsc{Formula}
:

By lemma
32
,
\sigma_{E}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\_
for some
t
. Let
V^{\prime}
be the valuation corresponding to this derivation. Then since this is a well-typed program, one of the following cases must apply:

Case 5(a).

V^{\prime}(t)=\texttt{true}
: Let
g^{\prime}=g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t
. Then
V(g^{\prime})=\texttt{true}
and by induction,
\sigma[g=g^{\prime}],\,\sigma_{E}[g=g^{\prime}]\vdash\phi_{1}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
where
V^{\prime\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
for the corresponding derivation
V^{\prime\prime}
with initial valuation
V^{\prime}
. Then by
SConsumeConditionalA
,
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
, and
V^{\prime\prime}
is the corresponding valuation for this derivation with initial valuation
V
.

Case 5(b).

V^{\prime}(t)=\texttt{false}
: Let
g^{\prime}=g(\sigma)\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}\mathop{\texttt{!}}t
. Then
V(g^{\prime})=\texttt{true}
and by induction,
\sigma[g=g^{\prime}],\,\sigma_{E}[g=g^{\prime}]\vdash\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
where
V^{\prime\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
for the corresponding derivation
V^{\prime\prime}
with initial valuation
V^{\prime}
. Then by
SConsumeConditionalB
,
\sigma,\,\sigma_{E}\vdash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~\phi_{1}~\texttt{else}~\phi_{2}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
, and
V^{\prime\prime}
is the corresponding valuation for this derivation with initial valuation
V
.

∎

Lemma 0 (Progress of consume (short form)).

For any heap
H
,
\sigma
,
\tilde{\phi}
, and valuation
V
, if
V(g(\sigma))=\texttt{true}
then
\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\_
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
such that
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
where
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation.

Proof.

By lemma
51
,
\sigma,\,\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},\,\allowbreak\_,\,\allowbreak\_
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
where
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
. Then by
SConsume
,
\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\_
, and
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation for this derivation.
∎

D.6.
Progress

Definition 0.

For a derivations
\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\Theta
, given an initial valuation
V
and heap
H
, the
corresponding valuation
is denoted as

V[\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\Theta\mid H].

This function is defined as follows, depending on the rule that proves the derivation. Values are referenced using the respective name from the rule definition.

•

SGuardInit
:

V[\mathsf{init}\rightharpoonup\langle\bot,\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,\texttt{true}\rangle\dashv\emptyset,\,\emptyset\mid H]:=V

•

SGuardSeq
:

V[\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{skip}\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma\dashv\emptyset,\,\emptyset\mid H]:=V

•

SGuardAssign
:

V[\langle\sigma,\,x=e\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\emptyset\mid H]:=V[\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow\_\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\mid H]

•

SGuardAssignField
:

\displaystyle V[\langle\sigma,\,x.f=e\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime},\,\emptyset\mid H]:=

\displaystyle\quad V[\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow\_\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\mid H][\sigma^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{acc}(x.f)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime}\mid H]

•

SGuardAlloc
:

V[\langle\sigma,\,x=\texttt{alloc}(S)\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma\dashv\emptyset,\,\emptyset\mid H]:=V

•

SGuardCall
:

\displaystyle V[\langle\sigma,\,y\mathbin{\texttt{=}}m(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)]\dashv\overline{\mathcal{R}}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime},\,\operatorname{rem}(\sigma^{\prime\prime},\operatorname{pre}(m))\mid H]:=

\displaystyle\quad V\overline{[\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\mid H]}[\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=[\overline{x\mapsto t}]]\vdash\operatorname{pre}(m)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\mid H]

•

SGuardAssert
:

V[\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{assert}~\phi\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\emptyset\mid H]:=V[\sigma\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}\mid H]

•

SGuardFold
:

\displaystyle V[\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{fold}~p(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)]\dashv\_,\,\emptyset\mid H]:=

\displaystyle\quad V\overline{[\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\mid H]}[\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=[\overline{x\mapsto t}]]\vdash\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\mid H]

•

SGuardUnfold
:

\displaystyle V[\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{unfold}~p(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\cup\bigcup\overline{\mathcal{R}},\,\emptyset\mid H]:=

\displaystyle\quad V\overline{[\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\mid H]}[\sigma^{\prime}\vdash p(\overline{e})\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\mid H]

•

SGuardIf
:

\displaystyle V[\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~s_{1}~\texttt{else}~s_{2}\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\emptyset\mid H]:=

\displaystyle\quad V[\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow\_\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\mid H]

•

SGuardWhile
:

\displaystyle V[\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime},\,\tilde{\phi}^{\prime}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime\prime})]\dashv\_,\,\_\mid H]:=

\displaystyle\quad V_{0}[\overline{t\mapsto V_{0}(\gamma(\sigma^{\prime})(x))}][\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma^{\prime})[\overline{x\mapsto t}]]\vdash\tilde{\phi}\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}\mid H]

where
V_{0}=V[\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\mid H]
.

•

SGuardFinish
:

V[\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{skip},\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\emptyset\mid H]:=V[\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}\mid H]

Lemma 0.

If
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma
, then
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\operatorname{rem}(\sigma^{\prime},\tilde{\phi}^{\prime})\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}
.

Proof.

Let
\alpha_{V}=V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\operatorname{rem}(\sigma^{\prime},\tilde{\phi}^{\prime})\rrparenthesis_{H}
. By lemma
12
it suffices to show that
(\alpha\setminus\alpha_{V})\subseteq\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}
. By lemma
4

\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}\subseteq\alpha
, therefore it suffices to show that
\alpha_{V}\cap\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}=\emptyset
.

If
\operatorname{pre}(m)
is completely precise,
\operatorname{rem}(\sigma^{\prime},\operatorname{pre}(m))=\emptyset
, thus
\alpha_{V}=\emptyset
.

Otherwise,

\displaystyle\alpha_{V}

\displaystyle=V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\operatorname{rem}(\sigma^{\prime},\tilde{\phi}^{\prime})\rrparenthesis_{H}

\displaystyle=V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\{\langle t,\,f\rangle:\langle f,\,t,\,t^{\prime}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)\}\cup\{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle:\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\}\rrparenthesis_{H}

\displaystyle=\bigcup_{\langle f,\,t,\,t^{\prime}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)}V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\langle f,\,t,\,t^{\prime}\rangle\rrparenthesis_{H}\cup\bigcup_{\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)}V\llparenthesis\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\rrparenthesis_{H}

\displaystyle=\bigcup_{h\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)}V\llparenthesis h\rrparenthesis_{H}

But since
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\alpha_{V},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
, for each
h\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma)\cup\mathcal{H}(\sigma)
,
V^{\prime}\llparenthesis h\rrparenthesis_{H}\cap\alpha_{E}=\emptyset
by lemma
15
. Therefore
\alpha_{V}\cap\alpha_{E}=\emptyset
.
∎

Theorem 55 (Progress, part 1).

Let
\Gamma
be some dynamic state validated by
\Sigma
and valuation
V
. If
\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\Theta
with corresponding valuation
V^{\prime}
extending
V
,
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
, and
\langle H,\,\alpha(\Gamma)\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
then

\Pi\vdash\Gamma,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H(\Gamma)}\to\Gamma^{\prime}

for some
\Gamma^{\prime}
.

In other words, if the dynamic state satisfies the matching symbolic checks, then dynamic execution can proceed.

Proof.

We procede by cases on
\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\Theta
.

Case 1.

SGuardInit
: Result is trival by
ExecInit
.

Case 2.

SGuardSeq
: Then
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{skip}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle
for some
H,\alpha,\rho,s,\mathcal{S}^{\prime}
, thus

\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{skip}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\emptyset\rrparenthesis_{H}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle
by
ExecSeq
, and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 3.

SGuardAssign
: Then
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,x=e\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle
for some
H,\alpha,\rho,\mathcal{S}^{\prime}
.

By
SGuardAssign

\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}
for some
t,\sigma^{\prime},\mathcal{R}
. Also,
V^{\prime}=V[\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\mid H]
. Since
\Gamma
corresponds to
\Sigma
, by definition
19

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma(\Sigma)
. By assumptions
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
, and
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
. Then
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t)
and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
by lemma
27
.

Therefore
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,x=e\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\emptyset\rrparenthesis_{H}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho[x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t)],\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle
by
ExecAssign
, and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 4.

SGuardAssignField
: Then
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,x.f=e\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle
for some
H,\alpha,\rho,x,f,e,s,\mathcal{S}^{\prime}
.

By
SGuardAssignField

\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}_{1}
for some
t,\sigma^{\prime},\mathcal{R}_{1}
, and
\sigma^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{acc}(x.f)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}_{2}
for some
\sigma^{\prime\prime},\mathcal{R}_{2}
. Also,
V^{\prime}=V[\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}_{1}\mid H][\sigma^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{acc}(x.f)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}_{2}\mid H]
.

Since
\Gamma
corresponds to
\Sigma
, by definition
19

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma(\Sigma)
. By assumptions
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\mathcal{R}_{2}
, thus
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}_{1}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}_{2}
by lemma
23
, and
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime\prime}))=\texttt{true}
, thus
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
by lemma
41
.

Now
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t)
and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
by lemma
27
. Let
\ell=\rho(x)
, then
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash x\Downarrow\rho(x)
by
EvalVar
. Finally,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{acc}(x.f)
by lemma
50
.

Let
H^{\prime}=H[\langle\ell,\,f\rangle\mapsto V^{\prime}(t)]
. Then
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,x.f=e\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\emptyset\rrparenthesis_{H}\to\langle H^{\prime},\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle
by
ExecAssignField
, and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 5.

SGuardAlloc
: Then
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,x=\texttt{alloc}(S)\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle
for some
H,\alpha,\rho,x,S,s,\mathcal{S}^{\prime}
.

Let
\ell=\operatorname{fresh}
,
\overline{T~f}=\operatorname{struct}(S)
, and
H^{\prime}=H[\overline{(\ell,f)\mapsto\operatorname{default}(T)}]
. Then

\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,x=\texttt{alloc}(S)\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\emptyset\rrparenthesis_{H}\to\langle H^{\prime},\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle
by
ExecAlloc
, and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 6.

SGuardCall
:
Then
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,y\mathbin{\texttt{=}}m(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\rangle\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}
for some
H,\alpha,\rho,y,m,\overline{e},s,\mathcal{S}
. Let
\overline{x}=\operatorname{params}(m)
.

By
SGuardCall

\overline{\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}}
for some
t,\sigma^{\prime},\mathcal{R}
, and
\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=[\overline{x\mapsto t}]]\vdash\operatorname{pre}(m)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}
for some
\sigma^{\prime\prime},\mathcal{R}^{\prime}
.

Also, by definition
V^{\prime}=V\overline{[\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\mid H]}[\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\overline{x\mapsto t}]\vdash\operatorname{pre}(m)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\mid H]
.

Since
\Gamma
corresponds to
\Sigma
, by definition
19

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma(\Sigma)
. By assumptions,
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime\prime}))=\texttt{true}
thus
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
by lemma
41
, and
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime}
, thus
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{\prime}
by lemma
23
.

Then
\overline{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t)}
,
\overline{\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e}
, and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
by lemma
27
.

Let
\gamma^{\prime}=[\overline{x\mapsto t}]
and
\rho^{\prime}=[\overline{x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t)}]
. Since
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
and
\rho^{\prime}\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\gamma^{\prime}
by construction,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}]
.

Let
\alpha_{V}=V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\operatorname{rem}(\sigma^{\prime},\operatorname{pre}(m))\rrparenthesis_{H}
and
\alpha_{E}=\llfloor\operatorname{pre}(m)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}
.

As noted before,
\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}]\vdash\operatorname{pre}(m)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}
by
SExecCall
. Also,
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime\prime}))=\texttt{true}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{\prime}
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\alpha_{E},\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\operatorname{pre}(m)
by lemma
50
.

Then
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\alpha_{V},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\operatorname{pre}(m)
by lemma
54
.

Let
\alpha^{\prime}=\lfloor\operatorname{pre}(m)\rfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\alpha_{V},\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle}
. Then
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,y\mathbin{\texttt{=}}m(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\alpha_{V}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho^{\prime},\,\operatorname{body}(m)\texttt{;}~\texttt{skip}\rangle\cdot\langle\alpha\setminus\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho,\,y\mathbin{\texttt{=}}m(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle
by
ExecCallEnter
, and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 7.

SGuardAssert
:
Then
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{assert}~\phi\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle
for some
H,\alpha,\rho,\phi,s,\mathcal{S}
.

By
SGuardAssert

\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}
, also
V^{\prime}=V[\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}\mid H]
.

Since
\Gamma
corresponds to
\Sigma
, by definition
19

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma(\Sigma)
. Also by assumptions,
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
, and
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
. Thus
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\phi
by lemma
50
since
\texttt{?}*\phi
is a specification.

Therefore
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{assert}~\phi\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\emptyset\rrparenthesis_{H}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle
by
ExecAssert
, and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 8.

SGuardFold
:
Then
s(\Sigma)=s(\Gamma)=\texttt{fold}~p(\overline{e})
for some
p,\overline{e}
. Thus
ExecFold
trivially applies, and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 9.

SGuardUnfold
:
Then
s(\Sigma)=s(\Gamma)=\texttt{unfold}~p(\overline{e})
for some
p,\overline{e}
. Thus
ExecUnfold
trivially applies, and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 10.

SGuardIf
:
Then
s(\Sigma)=s(\Gamma)=\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~s_{1}~\texttt{else}~s_{2}\texttt{;}~s
for some
e,s_{1},s_{2}
, and thus
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~s_{1}~\texttt{else}~s_{2}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle
for some
H,\alpha,\rho,\mathcal{S}
.

By
SGuardIf

\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}
for some
t,\sigma^{\prime},\mathcal{R}
, and also
V^{\prime}=V[\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}\mid H]
.

Now by assumptions
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
. Then
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t)
and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e
by lemma
27
.

Now, since we assume a well-typed program,
V^{\prime}(t)=\texttt{true}
or
false
. Then either
ExecIfA
or
ExecIfB
applies, and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 11.

SGuardWhile
:
Then
s(\Sigma)=s(\Gamma)=\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime}
for some
e
,
\tilde{\phi}
,
s
,
s^{\prime}
, and thus
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime}\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle
for some
H
,
\alpha
,
\rho
,
\mathcal{S}
.

Let
\overline{x}=\operatorname{modified}(s)
. By
SGuardWhile

\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}
,
\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma^{\prime})[\overline{x\mapsto\operatorname{fresh}}]]\vdash\tilde{\phi}\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}
, and
\sigma^{\prime\prime}\vdash e\downarrow t\dashv\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime}
. Then by definition
53

V^{\prime}
extends the corresponding valuation for these judgements.

By assumptions
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime\prime}))=\texttt{true}
, thus
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
by lemma
34
.

Also by assumptions
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime}
, thus
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{\prime}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime}
by lemma
23
.

Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}
.

Let
\hat{\alpha}=V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\operatorname{rem}(\sigma^{\prime},\tilde{\phi})\rrparenthesis_{H}
. Then by lemma
54

\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\hat{\alpha},\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}
.

Let
\overline{t}
be the list of fresh values used in
\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma^{\prime})[\overline{x\mapsto\operatorname{fresh}}]]\vdash\tilde{\phi}\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}
when applying
SGuardWhile
. Let
\gamma^{\prime}=\gamma(\sigma^{\prime})[\overline{x\mapsto t}]
. Then by definition
53
, and since
\rho\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\gamma(\sigma^{\prime})
, for each pair of
x
and
t
,
V^{\prime}(\gamma^{\prime}(x))=V^{\prime}(t)=V^{\prime}(\gamma(\sigma^{\prime})(x))=\rho(x)
.

Therefore
\rho\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\gamma^{\prime}
, and thus
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\tilde{\phi}\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}]
.

Now by lemma
36

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime\prime}
.

Also, as noted before,
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime}
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t)
by lemma
30
.

Let
\alpha^{\prime}=\llfloor\rho\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\hat{\alpha}\rangle}
.

Now, since we assume the program to be properly typed, one of the following subcases apply:

Case 11(a).

V^{\prime}(t)=\texttt{true}
: Then by
ExecWhileSkip

\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime}\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,\hat{\alpha}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho,\,s\texttt{;}~\texttt{skip}\rangle\cdot\langle\alpha\setminus\alpha^{\prime},\,\rho,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime}\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle
and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 11(b).

V^{\prime}(t)=\texttt{false}
: Then by
ExecWhileSkip

\langle\Pi,\,H\rangle,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime}\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\to\langle\hat{\alpha},\,H\rangle{\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}}
and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 12.

SGuardFinish
:
Then
s(\Sigma)=s(\Gamma)=\texttt{skip}
and thus
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{skip}\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}\rangle
for some
H,\alpha,\rho,\mathcal{S}
.

By
SGuardFinish

\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}
, and
V^{\prime}=V[\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}\mid H]
. By assumptions,
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
and
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)
.

Since
\Gamma
is a valid state, the partial state
\langle H,\,\mathcal{S}\rangle
must be validated by
\Sigma
and
V
, thus one of the following subcases must apply:

Case 12(a).

\mathcal{S}=\mathsf{nil}
– then
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{skip}\rangle\cdot\mathsf{nil}\rangle
. Then
ExecFinal
trivially applies to yield the result.

Case 12(b).

\mathcal{S}=\langle\alpha_{0},\,\rho_{0},\,m(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s_{0}\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}
for some
\alpha_{0},\rho_{0},m,\overline{e},s_{0},\mathcal{S}^{\prime}
and
\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)=\operatorname{post}(m)
.

Since
\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)=\operatorname{post}(m)
,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\operatorname{post}(m)
. Then
ExecCallExit
applies, and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

Case 12(c).

\mathcal{S}=\langle\alpha_{0},\,\rho_{0},\,\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s_{0}\texttt{;}~s_{0}^{\prime}\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{\prime}
for some
\alpha_{0},\rho_{0},e,\tilde{\phi},s_{0},s_{0}^{\prime},\mathcal{S}^{\prime}
and
\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)=\tilde{\phi}
.

Since
\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)=\operatorname{post}(m)
,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\tilde{\phi}
. Then
ExecWhileFinish
applies, and the result is immediate from
ExecStep
.

∎

Theorem 56 (Progress, part 2).

Let
\Gamma
be some well-formed dynamic state validated by
\Sigma
and valuation
V
. Then if
\Gamma\neq\mathsf{final}
,

\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime},\mathcal{R},\Theta

for some
\sigma^{\prime}
,
\mathcal{R}
,
\Theta
such that
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
where
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation extending
V^{\prime}
.

In other words, there is always some matching guard that computes the necessary checks.

Proof.

First, if
\Gamma=\mathsf{init}
, then
SGuardInit
applies to yield the desired result.

Otherwise,
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\mathcal{S}\rangle
for some non-empty stack. Therefore
\Sigma=\langle\sigma,\,s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle
for some
\sigma
,
s
, and
\tilde{\phi}
such that
\langle H,\,\alpha(\Gamma),\,\rho(\Gamma)\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma
and
s=s(\Gamma)
.

Then one of the following cases apply since
\mathcal{S}
is a well-formed stack:

Case 1.

s=\texttt{skip}
: By lemma
52

\sigma\vdash\tilde{\phi}\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
,
\mathcal{R}
such that
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
where
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation. Then by
SGuardFinish

\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\emptyset
and
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation for this judgement.

Case 2.

s=s^{\prime}\texttt{;}~s^{\prime\prime}
for some
s^{\prime}
,
s^{\prime\prime}
: We complete the proof by proving the following statement by induction on the syntax form of
s^{\prime}
:

If
s=s^{\prime}\texttt{;}~s^{\prime\prime}
then
\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime},\mathcal{R},\Theta
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
,
\mathcal{R}
,
\Theta
such that
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
where
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation extending
V^{\prime}
.

Case 2(a).

s^{\prime}=s_{1}\texttt{;}~s_{2}
:
By lemma
20
,
s^{\prime}=s_{1}^{\prime}\texttt{;}~s_{2}^{\prime}
where
s_{1}^{\prime}
is not a sequence statement. Then
s\texttt{;}~s^{\prime}=s_{1}^{\prime}\texttt{;}~s_{2}^{\prime}\texttt{;}~s^{\prime}
.

Then the inductive hypothesis applies, which completes the proof.

Case 2(b).

s^{\prime}=\texttt{skip}
:
Then
\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma\dashv\emptyset,\,\emptyset
by
SGuardSeq
.

Case 2(c).

s^{\prime}=x\mathbin{\texttt{=}}e
:
By lemma
28
,
\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow\_\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
and
\mathcal{R}
such that
V_{1}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
for the corresponding valuation
V_{1}
.

Then
\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\emptyset
by
SGuardAssign
. By definition the corresponding valuation extends
V_{1}
, thus
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
.

Case 2(d).

x.f\mathbin{\texttt{=}}e
:

By lemma
28

\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow\_\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}^{\prime}
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
and
\mathcal{R}^{\prime}
such that
V_{1}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
where
V_{1}
is the corresponding valuation extending
V
.

By lemma
52

\sigma^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{acc}(x.f)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime}
for some
\sigma^{\prime\prime}
and
\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime}
such that
V_{2}(g(\sigma^{\prime\prime}))=\texttt{true}
for corresponding valuation
V_{2}
, with initial valuation
V_{1}
.

Then
\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime},\,\emptyset
by
SGuardAssignField
. By definition the corresponding valuation
V^{\prime}
extends
V_{2}
, therefore
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime\prime}))=\texttt{true}
.

Case 2(e).

x=\texttt{alloc}(S)
:

Then
\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma\dashv\emptyset,\,\emptyset
by
SExecGuardAlloc
.

Case 2(f).

y\mathbin{\texttt{=}}m(e_{1},\cdots,e_{n})
:

Let
\sigma_{0}=\sigma
and
V_{0}=V
, then for each
e_{i}
,
\sigma_{i-1}\vdash e\Downarrow t_{i}\dashv\sigma_{i},\,\_
for some
\sigma_{i}
such that
V_{i}(g(\sigma_{i}))=\texttt{true}
for corresponding valuation
V_{i}
, with initial valuation
V_{i-1}
, by lemma
28
.

Let

. By lemma

,
\sigma_{n}[\gamma=[\overline{x_{i}\mapsto t_{i}}]]\vdash\operatorname{pre}(m)\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\_
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
such that
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
for corresponding valuation
V^{\prime}
, with initial valuation
V_{n}
.

Then
\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)]\dashv\mathcal{R}_{1}\cup\cdots\cup\mathcal{R}_{n}\cup\mathcal{R}^{\prime},\,\operatorname{rem}(\sigma^{\prime\prime},\operatorname{pre}(m))
by
SGuardCall
and
V^{\prime}
is the corresponding valuation

Case 2(g).

\texttt{assert}~\tilde{\phi}
:

for some

and

and

for some

,
\sigma^{\prime}

by

and

Case 2(i).

\texttt{while}~e~\texttt{invariant}~\tilde{\phi}~\texttt{do}~s
for some

,

,

.

Then by lemma

such that

.

for some

. Let

, thus

.

by

and

is the corresponding valuation.
