applies:

is satisfied.

Case 7.

ExecAssert
: We have

\displaystyle\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{assert}~\phi\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle

\displaystyle\text{where}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{?}*\phi.

Since the initial state is validated by
\Sigma
,
\Sigma=\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{assert}~\phi\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle
for some
\sigma
,
\tilde{\phi}
where
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma
.

The only guard rule that applies is
SGuardAssert
, so we have, for some
\sigma^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\sigma\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}

\displaystyle\text{and by assumptions }V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}\quad\text{and}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}.

Also, by definition
V^{\prime}=V[\sigma\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\mathcal{R}\mid H]
.

Thus by lemma
50

\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\texttt{?}*\phi\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
.

Thus by lemma
19
,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
. Also, as noted before,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash\texttt{?}*\phi
. Therefore, by lemma
36
, for some
\sigma^{\prime\prime}
,

\sigma^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{?}*\phi\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}\quad\text{and}\quad V^{\prime\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime\prime}))=\texttt{true}.

Now, by
SExecAssert
,
\sigma\vdash\texttt{assert}~\phi\texttt{;}~s\to s\dashv\sigma[g=g(\sigma^{\prime\prime})]
.

Let
\Sigma^{\prime}=\langle\sigma[g=g(\sigma^{\prime\prime})],\,s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle
. We want to show that
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle
is validated by
\Sigma^{\prime}
and
V^{\prime\prime}
.

By
SVerifyStep
,
\Sigma^{\prime}
is reachable from
\Pi
with valuation
V^{\prime\prime}
.

By assumptions,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma
, and

, thus

. Also, by definition,

. Therefore

corresponds to

with valuation
V^{\prime\prime}
.

Finally, by definition
\gamma(\Sigma^{\prime})=\gamma(\Sigma)
and
\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma^{\prime})=\tilde{\phi}=\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)
.

Therefore
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle
is a valid state by lemma
57
.

Case 8.

ExecIfA
: We have

\displaystyle\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~s_{1}~\texttt{else}~s_{2}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s_{1}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle

\displaystyle\text{where}\quad\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow\texttt{true}\quad\text{and}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{\mathrm{frm}}e

Since the initial state is validated by
\Sigma
,
\Sigma=\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~s_{1}~\texttt{else}~s_{2}\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle
for some
\sigma
,
\tilde{\phi}
where
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma
.

The only guard rule that applies is
SGuardIf
, so we have, for some

,

\displaystyle\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}

\displaystyle\text{and by assumptions}\quad V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}\quad\text{and}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}

where

.

Now by

,

\sigma\vdash\texttt{if}~e~\texttt{then}~s_{1}~\texttt{else}~s_{2}\texttt{;}~s\to s_{1}\texttt{;}~s\dashv\sigma^{\prime}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t].

,
\Sigma^{\prime}
is reachable from
\Pi
with valuation

.

Now
\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t)
and

, thus
V^{\prime}(t)=\texttt{true}
. Also,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
, thus

, and then

. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}[g=g(\sigma^{\prime})\mathbin{\texttt{\&\&}}t]
.

Also, by definition,

. Therefore
\Sigma^{\prime}
corresponds to

\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s_{1}\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle
with valuation

.

Finally,

by lemma

and

by definition.

Therefore

is a valid state by lemma

.

\displaystyle\text{and}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma_{1},\quad\cdots,\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma_{n}.

Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
.

Let
\gamma^{\prime}=[\overline{x\mapsto t}]
and
\rho^{\prime}=[\overline{x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t)}]
. Then, by construction,
\rho^{\prime}\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\gamma^{\prime}
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}]
.

From above we have
\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}]\vdash\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime}
and
\langle\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vdash_{H}\mathcal{R}^{\prime\prime}
by lemma
23
. Thus, by lemma
50

\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle},\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime\prime}
and thus

\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)].

Let
\mathsf{H}^{\prime}=\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime\prime});\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle
. Expanding definitions,

V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\rrparenthesis_{H}=\llfloor\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle}.

Now
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)]
, thus

\forall\,h_{1},h_{2}\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime\prime}):h_{1}\neq h_{2}\implies V^{\prime}\llparenthesis h_{1}\rrparenthesis_{H}\cap V^{\prime}\llparenthesis h_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H}=\emptyset

and by lemma

,

\forall\,h\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime\prime}):V^{\prime}\llparenthesis h\rrparenthesis_{H}\cap V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\rrparenthesis_{H}=\emptyset.

From these we can deduce that

\forall\,h_{1},h_{2}\in\mathsf{H}^{\prime}:h_{1}\neq h_{2}\implies V^{\prime}\llparenthesis h_{1}\rrparenthesis_{H}\cap V^{\prime}\llparenthesis h_{2}\rrparenthesis_{H}=\emptyset.

Also, from lemma

,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,[\overline{x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t)}]\rangle\vDash\operatorname{predicate}(p)
since
\rho^{\prime}=[\overline{x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t)}]
.

Since
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\rrparenthesis_{H},\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)]
,

\forall\,\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}(\sigma^{\prime\prime}):\langle H,\,\alpha,\,[\overline{x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t)}]\rangle\vDash\operatorname{predicate}(p).

From these we can deduce that

\forall\,\langle p,\,\overline{t}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}^{\prime}:\langle H,\,\alpha,\,[\overline{x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t)}]\rangle\vDash\operatorname{predicate}(p).

Since field values are unchanged between

\mathsf{H}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\forall\,\langle f,\,t,\,t^{\prime}\rangle\in\mathsf{H}^{\prime}:\langle V(t),\,f\rangle\in\alpha\quad\text{and}

Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\mathsf{H}^{\prime}
, and thus

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma),\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime}].

Let
\Sigma^{\prime}=\langle\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma),\mathsf{H}=\mathsf{H}^{\prime}],\,s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle
. By
SExecFold

\sigma\vdash\texttt{fold}~p(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\to s\dashv\sigma(\Sigma^{\prime})
(after expanding definitions). Therefore
\Pi\vdash\Sigma\to\Sigma^{\prime}
by
SVerifyStep
. Therefore
\Sigma^{\prime}
is reachable from
\Pi
with valuation
V^{\prime}
.

Also, as shown before,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma(\Sigma^{\prime})
, and by definition

. Therefore

corresponds to
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle
.

By definition

and

. Therefore by lemma

\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle

Case 14.

ExecUnfold
: We have

\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{unfold}~p(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H}\to\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle

By assumptions, the initial state is validated by some
\Sigma
and valuation
V^{\prime}
, thus

\Sigma=\langle\sigma,\,\texttt{unfold}~p(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle

,

where
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma
.

The only guard that applies is

, thus we have
The only guard that applies is
SGuardFold
, thus we have

\displaystyle\overline{\sigma\vdash e\Downarrow t\dashv\sigma^{\prime},\,\mathcal{R}},\quad\sigma^{\prime}\vdash p(\overline{e})\rhd\sigma^{\prime\prime},~\mathcal{R}^{\prime},

\displaystyle\text{and by assumptions}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{\prime}\cup\bigcup\overline{\mathcal{R}}\quad\text{and}\quad V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime\prime}))=\texttt{true}

Let
e_{1},\cdots,e_{n}=\overline{e}
,

, and
\mathcal{R}_{1},\cdots,\mathcal{R}_{n}=\overline{\mathcal{R}}
. Let
\sigma_{0}
, then for some

where

. By lemmas

. Also, by lemma

\displaystyle\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{1}\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t_{1}),\quad\cdots,\quad\langle H,\,\rho\rangle\vdash e_{n}\Downarrow V^{\prime}(t_{n})

\displaystyle\text{and}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma_{1},\quad\cdots,\quad\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma_{n}.

Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}
.

Thus by lemma
50

\langle H,\,\rho,\,\alpha\rangle\vDash p(\overline{e})\quad\text{and}\quad\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor p(\overline{e})\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle},\,.\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}

Let
\overline{x}=\operatorname{predicate\_params}(p)
,
\gamma^{\prime}=[\overline{x\mapsto t}]
, and
\rho^{\prime}=[\overline{x\mapsto V^{\prime}(t)}]
. Then, by construction,
\rho^{\prime}\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\gamma^{\prime}
. Therefore
\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor p(\overline{e})\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle},\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}]
.

Now, by definition,
\llfloor p(\overline{e})\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}=\llfloor\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle}\cup\bigcup\overline{\llfloor e\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}}
.

Therefore
\llfloor\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle}\subseteq\alpha\setminus\llfloor p(\overline{e})\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho\rangle}
, thus by lemma
19
,

\langle H,\,\alpha\setminus\llfloor\operatorname{predicate}(p)\rrfloor_{\langle H,\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle},\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}]

and
\langle\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}],\,H,\,\alpha\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\rule[0.75348pt]{13.14929pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime}}$}}}\hskip-13.14929pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[13.14929pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma{\rho^{\prime}}
.

Since
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\vDash p(\overline{e})
, by
AssertPredicate

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle\vDash\operatorname{predicate}(p)
.

Therefore by lemma
37

\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}]\vdash\operatorname{predicate}(p)\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}\quad\text{and}\quad V^{\prime\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime\prime}))=\texttt{true}

where
V^{\prime\prime}=V^{\prime}[\sigma^{\prime}[\gamma=\gamma^{\prime}]\vdash\operatorname{predicate}(p)\lhd\sigma^{\prime\prime}\mid H]
. Also, by lemma
36

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho^{\prime}\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{15.35417pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-15.35417pt\rule[0.75348pt]{15.35417pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-15.35417pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[15.35417pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime\prime}}$}}}\hskip-15.35417pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[15.35417pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime\prime}
, and thus

\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{15.35417pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-15.35417pt\rule[0.75348pt]{15.35417pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-15.35417pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[15.35417pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime\prime}}$}}}\hskip-15.35417pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[15.35417pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)].

Now, by
SExecUnfold
,
\sigma\vdash\texttt{unfold}~p(\overline{e})\texttt{;}~s\to s\dashv\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)]
.

Let
\Sigma^{\prime}=\langle\sigma^{\prime\prime}[\gamma=\gamma(\sigma)],\,s,\,\tilde{\phi}\rangle
. By

\Pi\vdash\Sigma\to\Sigma^{\prime}
. Therefore
\Sigma^{\prime}
is reachable from
\Pi
with valuation
V^{\prime\prime}
.

Also, as shown before,
\langle H,\,\alpha,\,\rho\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{15.35417pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-15.35417pt\rule[0.75348pt]{15.35417pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-15.35417pt\raisebox{-8.74913pt}{\makebox[15.35417pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V^{\prime\prime}}$}}}\hskip-15.35417pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[15.35417pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma(\Sigma^{\prime})
. Furthermore,

by definition. Thus
\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle

\Sigma^{\prime}
with valuation

.

By definition
\gamma(\Sigma^{\prime})=\gamma(\sigma)=\gamma(\Sigma)
and
\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma^{\prime})=\tilde{\phi}=\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)
. Therefore, by lemma
57

\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,s\rangle\cdot\mathcal{S}^{*}\rangle

∎

Theorem 69 (Preservation).

Let
\Gamma
be some dynamic state validated by the
\Sigma
and valuation
V
for some program
\Pi
. If
\Sigma\rightharpoonup\sigma^{\prime}\dashv\mathcal{R},\,\Theta
with corresponding valuation
V
extending
V^{\prime}
,
V^{\prime}(g(\sigma^{\prime}))=\texttt{true}
,
\langle H,\,\alpha(\Gamma)\rangle\vdash_{V^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}
, and
\Pi\vdash V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H(\Gamma)},\,\Gamma\to\Gamma^{\prime}

then
\Gamma^{\prime}
is a valid state.

In other words, if the dynamic state satisfies the matching symbolic checks, and dynamic execution procedes, then the resulting state is valid.

Proof.

We proceed by cases on the judgement
\Pi\vdash V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H(\Gamma)},\,\Gamma\to\Gamma^{\prime}
.

Case 1.

ExecInit
: Then
\Gamma=\mathsf{init}
and
\Gamma^{\prime}=\langle\emptyset,\,\langle\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,s(\Pi)\rangle\cdot\mathsf{nil}\rangle
. Since
\Gamma
is validated by
\Sigma
, then
\Sigma=\mathsf{init}
.

Let
\Sigma^{\prime}=\langle\langle\bot,\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,\emptyset,\,\texttt{true}\rangle,\,s(\Pi),\,\texttt{true}\rangle
. Then by
SVerifyInit

\Pi\vdash\mathsf{init}\to\Sigma^{\prime}
.

Since
\Gamma^{\prime}
has a stack with a sole stack frame, and clearly
\Sigma^{\prime}
is reachable from
\Pi
, in order to show that
\Gamma^{\prime}
is validated by
\Sigma^{\prime}
it suffices to show that
\Gamma^{\prime}
corresponds to
\Sigma^{\prime}
. In turn, since clearly
s(\Gamma^{\prime})=s(\Sigma^{\prime})
, it suffices to show that
\langle H(\Gamma^{\prime}),\,\alpha(\Gamma^{\prime}),\,\rho(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{3.44444pt}{$\rule[-6.45831pt]{0.47787pt}{12.91663pt}\rule[-1.23135pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\rule[0.75348pt]{10.44441pt}{0.47787pt}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{-7.73688pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{V}$}}}\hskip-10.44441pt\raisebox{2.95354pt}{\makebox[10.44441pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}$}}}\sigma(\Sigma^{\prime})
. Since all the requisite sets or values are trivial, this is immediate from the definition.

Case 2.

ExecFinal
: Then
\Gamma^{\prime}=\mathsf{final}
and
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\langle\alpha,\,\rho,\,\texttt{skip}\rangle\cdot\mathsf{nil}\rangle
. Since
\Gamma
is validated by
\Sigma
,
s(\Gamma)=s(\Sigma)=\texttt{skip}
. By lemma
52
,
\sigma(\Sigma)\vdash\tilde{\phi}(\Sigma)\rhd\sigma^{\prime},~\_
for some
\sigma^{\prime}
. Thus
\Pi\vdash\Sigma\to\mathsf{final}
by
SVerifyFinal
.

Case 3.

ExecStep
: Then
\Gamma=\langle H,\,\mathcal{S}\rangle
and
\Gamma^{\prime}=\langle H^{\prime},\,\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle
for some
H,\mathcal{S},H^{\prime},\mathcal{S}^{\prime}
where

\langle H,\,\mathcal{S}\rangle,\,V^{\prime}\llparenthesis\Theta\rrparenthesis_{H}\to\langle H^{\prime},\,\mathcal{S}^{\prime}\rangle
. Therefore
\Gamma^{\prime}
is a valid state by lemma
68
.

∎
